# ARCHITECTURE — file-by-file layout

Extracted from `CLAUDE.md` (2026-06-04) to keep that file's always-loaded payload lean. This is the detailed map of where everything lives; the *why* behind structural choices stays in the CLAUDE.md Decisions log. **Append a new file / component / route here.** When the TOP-LEVEL shape changes, keep the high-level summary in CLAUDE.md's "File layout" section in sync.

## File layout

```
src/
├── app/
│   ├── layout.tsx                         next/font (Inter + Source Serif 4), sonner Toaster, Vercel Analytics, no-FOUC theme bootstrap, full SEO Metadata + Twitter card. Wraps the app in `CartProvider` + `BookmarksProvider` (saved-questions state, migration 0047) + `MobilePromptProvider` (soft mobile-capture nudge, 2026-07-12).
│   ├── favicon.ico · icon.png · apple-icon.png   brand V icons (2026-10-04); Next file conventions emit the <link> tags. GENERATED by `python scripts/brand/build_icons.py` from `assets/brand/v-mark-source.png` (the V, transparent) + `v-mark-16.svg` (pageless 16 px), which also writes public/icons/ (manifest 192/512/maskable, push-192 + push-badge-96 for sw.js, mark-96 for the header). Edit the sources and re-run, never the outputs
│   ├── opengraph-image.tsx                edge-runtime ImageResponse — dynamic 1200x630 OG image with the headline + LWS Pune mark
│   ├── robots.ts                          MetadataRoute.Robots — allow public routes (incl. /guide and /notes), disallow /dashboard /upload /questions /api
│   ├── sitemap.ts                         MetadataRoute.Sitemap — entries:
│   │       + /browse + /login
│   │       + /guide + /guide/nda landing
│   │       + nda-maths landing + 5 sections + **11 principle deep-dive pages**
│   │       + nda-english landing + 5 sections + **16 playbook detail pages** pulled from PLAYBOOK_SLUGS
│   │       + nda-physics landing + 7 sections (incl. ncert-map) + **14 chapter playbook detail pages** pulled from PHYSICS_PLAYBOOK_SLUGS
│   │       + nda-chemistry landing + 6 sections + **12 chapter playbook detail pages** pulled from CHEMISTRY_PLAYBOOK_SLUGS
│   │       + nda-biology landing + 6 sections + **9 chapter playbook detail pages** pulled from BIOLOGY_PLAYBOOK_SLUGS
│   │       + nda-geography landing + 5 sections + **7 chapter playbook detail pages** pulled from GEOGRAPHY_PLAYBOOK_SLUGS
│   │       + nda-history landing + 5 sections + **4 chapter playbook detail pages** pulled from HISTORY_PLAYBOOK_SLUGS
│   │       + nda-polity landing + 5 sections + **4 chapter playbook detail pages** pulled from POLITY_PLAYBOOK_SLUGS
│   │       + **nda-economics landing only** (single-page, no sub-routes, priority 0.7)
│   │       + **nda-current-affairs landing only** (single-page Template D, no sub-routes, priority 0.6)
│   │       + every /notes route (subject index landings + all 34 chapter landings + their subtopic pages, auto-derived from the `NOTES_CHAPTERS` registry so future notes auto-register)
│   ├── not-found.tsx                      branded 404 with AppHeader + Footer + "Browse questions" CTA
│   ├── error.tsx                          global error boundary with "Try again" + "Browse questions"; logs error.digest
│   ├── login/page.tsx                     email + password sign-in (client) — split-screen layout + Google button + sign-up link
│   ├── signup/page.tsx                    self-serve student registration (client): GoogleSignInButton + email/password+confirm; "check your email" fallback if confirmation is left on. Confirm-email is OFF, so signUp returns a live session → /browse.
│   ├── pricing/page.tsx + PricingClient   public pricing page (force-dynamic). One card per ACTIVE row of `public.plans` (listActivePlans; `?plan=<url_key>` highlights one); the free-mock sentence reads `free_mock_limit()` and says nothing when the limit is off. PricingClient loads Razorpay hosted checkout.js, POSTs /api/billing/order → opens Checkout → /api/billing/verify → /account. Anon → "Sign in to buy"; entitled (scope covered) → "You already have this".
│   ├── account/page.tsx                   signed-in (force-dynamic, noindex): premium status + expiry from loadEntitlements; staff show "included", else active grant or "See premium". Reached from the UserMenu "Account & access" item.
│   ├── performance/page.tsx               **the student's OWN performance diagnosis (2026-09-18, migration 0110)** — signed-in, `force-dynamic`, noindex; redirects anon → /login?next=/performance. Reads `getOwnPerformance()` (their own JWT through the zero-argument `get_own_performance()` RPC) and renders the SAME `components/performance/PerformanceBody` the superadmin route renders, with `viewer="self"`. Not two pages, because every number and caveat is the same claim whoever reads it and this repo has paid twice for a contract with two renderers; `viewer` changes SECOND PERSON ONLY and must never gate a figure — a student seeing a kinder subset of their own diagnosis than their teacher sees is how a readout stops being trusted. The student gets the WHOLE thing by decision: CLAUDE.md's engagement gate makes metacognition a principle ("every metric we track is shown to the student in plain language"), and they are the only person who can act on it. Reached from the UserMenu "Your performance" link and from every mock-report email. **Empty state (2026-10-06):** a student with no graded paper sees `components/performance/StarterCard.tsx`, one test picked by `lib/performance/starter.ts` (`pickStarter`, TDD `tests/performance-starter.test.ts`: the last practised chapter's test, else the target exam's first chapter test, else its newest past paper, else "not live yet", else the exam picker), fed by `lib/performance/starterQuery.ts` (the hourly-cached catalogue plus two own-row reads). Growth registry "performance-starter".
│   ├── drill/                             **the weak-area drill (2026-09-19)** — the first student-facing reader `answer_wrong` has ever had. Signed-in, `force-dynamic`, noindex; `page.tsx` (shell + empty state) around `DrillRunner.tsx` (client). **NO MIGRATION and NO NEW TABLE**: `drill_completed` + `answer_correct` were already in the 0052 CHECK (emitterless, on the PMF test's UNBUILT list, now empty), and a question's state is a FOLD over its own ordered wrong/correct events rather than a stored column — a `drill_state` field would be a second copy of an answer the log already gives. Pure core `lib/drill/select.ts` (`questionState` ladder — wrong → due · one correct → sleeps `COOL_DOWN_DAYS`=10 · second correct → retired · wrong again → back, streak reset, because a 4-option MCQ carries a 25% guessing floor; `dueQuestions`; `attachRefs`, where ABSENCE from the taxonomy map is the eligibility gate — the log outlives a question flipped PRIVATE; `selectDrill`, a TWO-LEVEL round-robin: chapters outside, subtopics within a chapter's turn. One level was wrong and `drill:smoke` is what found it — grouping on the (chapter, subtopic) pair let a live student draw 3 of 5 from Grammar, five distinct subtopics and plainly blocked practice, while the unit test asserting five distinct SUBTOPICS passed). Reads `lib/drill/query.ts` (paged `user_activity`; a DELIBERATELY NARROW taxonomy select over the whole pool — `queryQuestionsByIds` would drag `text`/`solution` through an ordering, the documented 13.9 MB sort-payload trap; `loadDrillQuestions` preserves the caller's order, since that order IS the interleaving). **The payload carries NO key and NO solution** (the `getPublicQuizBySlug` posture): grading is server-side in `POST /api/drill/answer`, because the verdict becomes the `user_activity` row that decides whether the question ever returns — a client-asserted one would let a browser retire its own questions. `POST /api/drill/complete` emits the feature event and deliberately records NO score (a client tally has no business beside server-graded rows; the score is derivable from them). `logActivity` stays best-effort and that is SAFE here: a lost write leaves the question DUE, so the failure direction is extra practice, never a wrongly-retired question. **Mobile-first** — one question per screen, full-width option targets, the single forward control pinned to the viewport bottom, no palette; an answer cannot be changed once committed. Entry points are `/me`, the mock-result findings card and the UserMenu (all `prefetch={false}`); deliberately NOT a primary-nav tab, which is public and most traffic is anon. Auth-gated `ƒ` AND click-gated, so doubly unprovable from the build: `npm run drill:smoke` drives the read chain on live data. **Attempt scope (2026-09-24, ENGAGEMENT_SPEC.md A1):** `/drill?attempt=<uuid>` narrows the pool to ONE sitting's wrong answers via `scopeToAttempt` (an INTERSECTION with the due pool, never a widening: a question fixed since stays out) — `getOwnDrill({ attemptId })` regrades the attempt through `regradeAttempt` (which now also returns the mock's slug + title for the header) and a non-owned or missing attempt degrades to the general drill rather than 404ing. `getOwnLadder(db, userId)` (due pool + fixed count, 2026-10-04; it replaced `getOwnDuePool`) folds the same `loadOwnPool` read the drill serves from for `GET /api/me/pulse`, so the count a student sees is the count the drill serves from. **The daily-set FILL (B2, 2026-09-24):** when an unscoped drill is short of five, `compose.ts`'s `fillUnseen` tops it up with UNSEEN PYQ MCQs — `rankWeakSubtopics` (fill.ts, the two subtopics with the most recorded misses, from EVERY miss not just the due ones) → `loadUnseenCandidates` by subtopic → then by the primary target exam (`getOnboardingState` + `getExamIdMap`); `loadSeenQuestionIds` (attempt_answers + activity refs, own-row, paged) is the filter and what moves the pool forward; `composeDailySet` keeps due first, dedupes, caps at five. `ServedQuestion.isNew` renders a "New" chip; a correct answer to a new question logs `question_practiced` (surface drill) via `hasPriorWrong`, never a false recovery. `compose.ts` carries no `server-only` so `drill:smoke` drives the same chain, and now also probes one SHORT-POOL student with the NDA fallback. TDD `tests/drill-fill.test.ts`. See [[project-engagement-engine]].
│   ├── me/WeekStrip.tsx + lib/goals/ + lib/pulse/ + lib/viewer/usePulse.ts + api/me/pulse/   **engagement Tranche A (2026-09-24, ENGAGEMENT_SPEC.md A2–A3).** `GET /api/me/pulse` → `{ due, week: { done, goal } }` (signed-in, `no-store`; `due` from `getOwnLadder`, `week` from `lib/goals/service.ts` — a `count: exact, head: true` over `user_activity` since Monday 00:00 IST, so the 1000-row cap cannot touch it). Pure core `lib/goals/weekly.ts` (`weekStartIst` · `WEEK_SITTING_KINDS` = mock_submitted | drill_completed | note_checkpoint | quiz_taken, finished things only · `weeklyProgress` · `weeklyGoalSentence`, growth-framed · `sanitizeWeeklyGoal` 1..14; TDD `tests/goals-weekly.test.ts`) + `lib/pulse/cache.ts` (`isPulseFresh` 10-min TTL · `parsePulseEntry` shape guard; TDD `tests/pulse-cache.test.ts`). `usePulse` (client) mirrors `useViewerSession`: one in-flight promise per page load, a sessionStorage entry across loads, and `invalidatePulse()` at the two moments the number changes (a drill answer in `DrillRunner`, a graded mock via `result/PulseRefresh.tsx`), pushing the fresh value to every mounted subscriber. Surfaces: a count badge on the UserMenu AVATAR (works at every width; deliberately NOT a new nav tab — the audience is on phones where the tab bar is a fixed five), `N due` on the menu's Fix row + `This week x of y` under the email, and `WeekStrip` on `/me` (ring + sentence + goal `<select>` PATCHing `/api/profile.weeklyGoal` + the one action, `Fix N mistakes` or `Take a mock`) which REPLACED the count-less DrillCard. Migration 0113 adds `student_profiles.weekly_goal` (nullable = not chosen; the default 3 is shown as a suggestion, never written). `lib/mocks/resultHeadline.ts` (`buildResultHeadline`, TDD `tests/mock-result-headline.test.ts`) is the result page's new headline: accuracy on ATTEMPTED + the unanswered count, never a share of max marks; `primaryAction` fix | another | retake drives the card's one lead button. **Due-queue nudge (C2, 2026-09-24, migration 0114):** `lib/email/dueNudge.ts` pure core (`selectDueNudges` — total, ordered skip reasons not-a-student · no-email · opted-out · nothing-due · drilled-recently · already-today · nudged-recently · backed-off (NUDGE_MAX_UNANSWERED=3 nudges with no drill since, until they drill again); `summarizeDue`; `dueNudgeDedupeKey` = `due_nudge:<userId>:<IST day>`; TDD `tests/email-due-nudge.test.ts`) · `lib/email/dueNudgeService.ts` (`readDueCandidates` — ONE paged pass over `user_activity` ordered `(created_at, id)`, folded with the drill's own `dueQuestions`, taxonomy resolved once over the union; `readNudgeConversion`) · `buildDueNudgeEmail` in `templates.ts` (content-led subject, never absence-led) · runner `scripts/email/send-due-nudge.ts` (`npm run email:due-nudge`, dry-run default, `--apply` only in `.github/workflows/due-nudge.yml` at 07:00 UTC) — see ENGAGEMENT_SPEC.md C2. **Mastery map (B1, 2026-09-24):** `me/map/page.tsx` (auth-gated `ƒ`, noindex; exam/subject pills from `buildLaneNav`, all `prefetch={false}`; native `<details>` tiles, two columns below sm) over `lib/performance/masteryMap.ts` (`bandOf` — unknown under `MIN_JUDGED_FOR_CLAIM`, else weak < `WEAK_BELOW` ≤ mid < `MASTERED_AT` ≤ mastered, the accordion's own constants so the two surfaces cannot disagree; `buildMasteryMap` — tiles worst-first, per-tile counts + plain-words label, `goPracticeHref` per dot; TDD `tests/performance-mastery-map.test.ts`); `npm run map:smoke` drives the chain on live data. Entry links: `/me` MockCard + the drill end screen (which now says "See your map"). **Teacher-assigned deadlines (C1, 2026-09-24, migration 0115 `mock_assignments`):** pure core `lib/assignments/core.ts` (`validateAssignmentInput` · `assignmentState` · `dueLabel` in IST · `completionFor` — any GRADED attempt of that mock by a roster student, total over the roster · `studentAssignmentViews`; TDD `tests/assignments-core.test.ts`) · `lib/assignments/service.ts` (staff paths through the caller's RLS client — `listBatchAssignments` / `createAssignment` (23505 → "already assigned", 42501 → "cannot assign here") / `deleteAssignment` / `readCompletionAttempts` via the 0083 attempts policy; the student path `listMyAssignments` is service-role scoped to one user id, the `listMyBatches` precedent, because a student cannot read `batches` for the name) · `POST /api/batches/assign` (requireEditor, then RLS) · `dashboard/batches/[id]/roster/AssignmentsCard.tsx` (client: picker + datetime-local + note; per row "N of M sat" and a `<details>` of who has not — NO score, NO ordering of students) · `/me` `DueList` (unsat first, soonest first, max 3, only when any exist) · one line under the title on `/mock/[slug]`. RLS pinned by `tests/mock-assignments-rls.test.ts` (enrolled reads, unenrolled does not, other-branch teacher cannot write or delete, org admin can). **Exam date (C3, 2026-09-24, migration 0116 `student_profiles.exam_date`):** `lib/exam/calendar.ts` — `EXAM_CALENDAR` (15 sittings, every one `official: false` as written because nothing for 2027 was announced; `source` says so) · `daysUntilIst` · `nextSitting` · `resolveExamDate` (future override wins and is official by definition; else the first target exam with an entry; a PAST override falls back) · `examCountdownSentence` ("… in 206 days (expected)") · `sanitizeExamDate`; TDD `tests/exam-calendar.test.ts`, which is ALSO the rot probe: a sitting more than `CALENDAR_GRACE_DAYS`=14 past FAILS the gate, the roadmap's stated preference over a DB table nothing checks. Surfaces: the pulse carries `exam` (optional, so an older cached entry still parses), rendered as one line in UserMenu and in the `/me` WeekStrip with a "set your date" link when not official; `/account` gets a date input whose hint names the expected date. `PATCH /api/profile` accepts `examDate` (null clears; a non-date is a 400).
│   ├── lib/celebrate/ + components/celebrate/ + lib/daily/ + lib/drill/progress.ts   **celebrations (2026-10-04, ENGAGEMENT_SPEC.md §6, migration 0132).** Pure: `runs.ts` (`stepRun` — first acts only, a miss resets silently, levels 3/5/10/every 10), `milestones.ts` (`highestMilestone` 10/20/50/every 50, `milestoneEvent` with dedupe key `milestone:answered:<user>:<n>`), `drill/progress.ts` (`answerProgress` wrong/rested/fixed/right + copy), `fixedCount` in `drill/select.ts`, `daily/questionOfDay.ts` (FNV-1a of `<exam>:<IST day>`), `totalsLine` in `pulse/cache.ts`. Server: `celebrate/service.ts` (`getOwnAnswerTotals` over the SECURITY INVOKER RPC `get_own_answer_totals`; `awardAnsweredMilestone` — upsert-ignore + select, so it returns a milestone only when THIS call wrote it), `daily/service.ts` (the day's id per exam via `unstable_cache` + service role, PUBLIC rows only). Client: `components/celebrate/celebrate.ts` (routes each message to V: `registerVSays` / `celebrate(text, face)`, held 800 ms for V to mount, else one sonner toast id), `components/chat/VSays.tsx` (V's bubble inside ChatWidget's corner, taps pass through, 3 s; sets `data-v-says` so VHello steps aside), `answerRun.ts` (module-level run + `seen` set, fed by `useAttemptReveal` on every allowed reveal), `CelebrateOnMount.tsx` (the mock result page's server-awarded milestone). Practice route: grades `isGradedSurface` (bank · board · daily) and returns 200 `{ milestone }` when the body asks (`celebrate: true`, debounced fetch flushes only, never the page-hide beacon). `recordDrillAnswer` returns `progress`/`fixedTotal`/`milestone`. **Beat the crowd** (same day): pure `crowd.ts` (`crowdTier` 70/80/90 at n >= 20 · `poolFreshStats` — only rows measured on the current hash and without `verdict_mismatch` · `isCrowdEligible` — a crowd-run review, verdict confirmed/fixed, hash unchanged, source not on the not-credible list) + `crowdService.ts` (service role, reviews first so the common path is one empty query); the practice route and `recordDrillAnswer` return `crowd`; next review candidates: `scripts/reviews/crowd-candidates.ts`; the first run: `scripts/reviews/apply-crowd-hard-fixes.ts`. Tests: `celebrate-runs`, `celebrate-milestones`, `celebrate-crowd`, `drill-progress`, `question-of-day`, `practice-route-verdict.integration`, `drill-answer-progress.integration`.
│   ├── feedback/page.tsx   **`/feedback`, the one address for "tell us something" (2026-09-27).** Renders the SAME `FeatureCard` as `/me` (exported from `me/FeedbackCards.tsx`, `defaultOpen`), so the two cannot drift; anon gets a sign-in link + the contact mailto instead of a form that would 401. Linked from the Footer ("Send feedback") and the UserMenu. `force-dynamic`, noindex. Admin side: `/dashboard/feedback` now leads with the cross-mock rating rollup (`summarizeMockRatings` in `lib/mocks/feedback.ts`), which before this was visible only one mock at a time.
│   ├── start/page.tsx + components/HowItWorks.tsx + lib/education/howItWorks.ts + lib/email/{welcome,welcomeService}.ts + scripts/email/send-welcome.ts + .github/workflows/welcome.yml   **student education (2026-09-24, STUDENT_EDUCATION_SPEC.md, migration 0117).** Measured first: 149 of 193 mock-takers had used a mock and NOTHING else, 2 had ever finished a drill — the features were unreachable in the student's head, not unwanted. `lib/education/howItWorks.ts` is the pure core: `loopFor(exam)` picks the three-step loop from REGISTRY flags (`hasMocks` → sit a real past paper `/mock/exam/<slug>` · fix what you missed `/drill` · watch your map fill `/me/map`; else the bank loop → the book reader `/board/<slug>` (or `/browse` for a non-board practice exam) · attempt before you reveal · save what you will revisit `/saved`; mocks win over `boardExam`) and `welcomeDestination(next, loop)` keeps a SPECIFIC `?next=` as the primary button with the loop entry secondary (TDD `tests/how-it-works.test.ts`). `HowItWorks.tsx` = numbered cards, `prefetch={false}` on every step (per-user renders). `/start` is static + indexable (revalidate 86400): both loops, one line per tab, the account-menu explanation; linked from Footer ("How it works", every page, so anon phone readers reach it), UserMenu (every width), the `/me` hero, `/about` and the sitemap. `welcome/ExamOnboarding.tsx` gained a SECOND SCREEN: after Continue/Skip it renders the chosen exam's loop + the two `welcomeDestination` buttons instead of navigating; `onboarded_at` still stamps on screen 1, so closing the tab on screen 2 costs the loop once, never a second ask. The result page carries a caption under "Fix these mistakes" in step 2's words (container `sm:flex-wrap`). **Welcome email:** `welcome.ts` (`selectWelcomes` — total; skips no-email · opted-out · already-welcomed · mailed-today (24 h after ANY other kind); picks NEWEST FIRST so `--limit` never leaves today's signup behind the backlog; `welcomeDedupeKey` = `welcome:<userId>`, NO day, so one-ever is the table's property; TDD `tests/email-welcome.test.ts`) · `welcomeService.ts` (`readPrimaryExams` paged over `student_profiles`, `isExamSlug`-guarded; `readWelcomeConversion` = any activity within 48 h, under-reports by design) · `buildWelcomeEmail` in `templates.ts` renders the SAME loop objects · runner `send-welcome.ts` (`npm run email:welcome`, dry-run default, `--sample-to` writes no row) · `welcome.yml` at 03:30 UTC = 09:00 IST, `--apply --limit=100`. The backlog of existing accounts is INCLUDED on purpose (373 of 379 on the first dry run). **Slice 2 (same day): the teacher channel.** `lib/education/classroomScript.ts` holds the five-minute script as data (`MOCK_SCRIPT` · `BOARD_SCRIPT` · `INVITE_LINES` · `classroomScriptText()` for the clipboard); the spec's appendix A is a MIRROR pinned by `tests/classroom-script.test.ts`. `dashboard/batches/[id]/roster/ClassroomScriptCard.tsx` (server `<details>`, collapsed, directly under the assignment control) + `CopyScriptButton.tsx` (the one island: clipboard + toast, "Copy for WhatsApp", because that is where a batch lives). `buildBatchInviteEmail` gains the three `INVITE_LINES` after the consent paragraph — generic by design, so the invite stays THIN (no name, ignore path kept, no unsubscribe).
│   ├── saved/page.tsx                     signed-in (noindex), redirects anon → /login?next=/saved. Reads the user's saved-question ids (`listBookmarkIds`, RLS own-row) → `queryQuestionsByIds`, re-sorts to bookmark order, renders the same `/browse` `QuestionCard` list. Reached from the UserMenu "Saved questions" link. (migration 0047; 2026-07-12)
│   ├── nda/                               public exam-home (Tier 1 Phase 2 ship). Hero + live `getExamHomeStats` total + dual CTA (Build a paper · All nine guides) + Strategy-guides grid (9 compact cards) + Teaching-notes grid (Statistics + Vectors) + closing "back to bank" banner. ISR `revalidate = 3600`, cookie-free anon client, full Metadata + `GuideJsonLd` CollectionPage. `/nda/opengraph-image` is an edge-runtime ImageResponse (purple gradient distinct from /guide's blue). Sitemap entry priority 0.95.
│   ├── board/                             book-faithful textbook-solutions reader for board exams (migration 0043; 2026-07-06). `/board` (exam index) → `/board/[examSlug]` (subject/chapter index; `listBoardChapters` range-paginated for the 1000-row cap) → `/board/[examSlug]/[subjectRoute]/[chapterSlug]` → `BoardReader` (client). Renders each chapter in PHYSICAL BOOK ORDER via the `section_*` axis (§ Solved Examples → Exercise → Miscellaneous), ORTHOGONAL to the conceptual `subtopic` used by /browse+/notes (see Design axes). Collapsible section/block accordion; attempt-first per-question reveal (metered via `useRevealMeter`, shared with /browse); `breakSentences` sentence-per-line; answers via `BlockText`. Data layer `lib/board/query.ts`; always-visible **Board** nav tab (`boardExam` flag). Data-driven — a chapter appears once its rows carry `section_seq` (gated by `npm run board:lint`). **The recurrence strip counts questions per PAPER, not per year (2026-09-23)** — pure core `lib/board/papers.ts` (`paperKeyFor` · `boardPyqPaperStats`, TDD `tests/board-papers.test.ts`), fed by `getSubjectPaperCounts` in `query.ts`. A year is a paper on only ONE of the three boards: CBSE Class 12 sets 5-6 a year and MH HSC 12 Mathematics has sat two since 2024, so those bars read ~6x and ~2x what one paper asks (CBSE Current Electricity: 42/37/50/36 per year, a steady 4.2/4.2/4.8/5.2 per paper). **The key is `source_file`, not `(pyq_year, pyq_month)`** — the month is parsed from the source compilation's provenance tag and tags like `[Q. 27, 2025]` carry none, so a NULL means the SOURCE never stated it and no repair is possible from here; on CBSE it is NULL on all 5,012 rows by construction. Two traps the rule encodes: a CBSE `-2`/`-3` file is a DELTA of its `-1` opener (dedup already stored the shared questions there), so it is excluded from the average and the strip says how many rows that is; and the divisor is a property of the SUBJECT, so dividing by the papers that happen to ask about a chapter turns Magnetism and Matter's 0.7 into 2.0 — `boardPyqPaperStats` REQUIRES the count rather than deriving a plausible wrong one. `npm run board:smoke-pyqs` checks the rule against what each board is independently known to set (CBSE 5-6, MH HSC 12 1-2, MH SSC 10 exactly 1). **Hub tabs (2026-10-07):** `/board/[examSlug]` shows subjects as tabs with chapters listed straight away (`_components/BoardHubTabs`, book order, no running chapter numbers since some books restart at 1 in Part II) plus `BoardContinueCard` (the chapter and section of the newest of the student's last 20 board answers in this exam; pure `lib/board/hub.ts`). See [[board-reader]].
│   ├── mock/                              timed PYQ mock tests (migration 0044; 2026-07-10) — real past papers served whole, native to pyqvault.com. `/mock` (catalogue) → `/mock/[slug]` (instructions + staff-only share link, `getSessionMember`-gated) → `/mock/[slug]/attempt/[id]` (`MockRunner` — refresh-resistant timer from `expires_at` → auto-submit · question palette · flag · debounced autosave · resume) → `/mock/attempt/[id]/result` (score + per-section + per-question review reusing the /browse render path; **gated by the `student_profiles` mobile-capture step** `MobileGate`, migration 0045). Signed-in students only (start → 401 anon). Pure core `lib/mocks/` (blueprints · reconstruct · attempt grade/timer/palette · perf, all TDD); `service.ts` = attempt lifecycle. Admin `/dashboard/mocks` (per-mock perf → per-attempt drill-down) + `/dashboard/students` (self-serve roster). **Navigated by TYPE since 2026-09-07 (migration 0088)**: `/mock` (exam picker) → `/mock/exam/[examSlug]` (since 2026-10-07 lists each type's 5 newest under tabs, `_components/MockHubTabs` + the signed-in `NextMockCard` (Resume, else the newest past paper not sat), pure core `lib/mocks/hub.ts`; "Show all" goes to the leaf, so the long list stays off the hub. It was a TYPE picker before that) → `/mock/exam/[examSlug]/[type]` (the leaf list). The exam page used to BE the leaf list, grouped by year; that breaks twice once an exam has more than one kind of mock — NDA is already 36 cards under 10 year headings, and an assembled paper has no sitting to head a group with. Type is derived from TWO independent columns (`source` = pyq|practice, `scope` = full|sectional) rather than one enum, because both combinations already exist as printed papers in `papers`; navigation follows `scope` first (a three-hour commitment vs a thirty-minute one) and `source` renders as a badge only where the two mix (sectional lists + `/mock/attempts`). `lib/mocks/catalogue.ts` is the pure core (`MOCK_TYPES` · `mockTypeOf` · `buildMockTypeCards` · `groupMocksForType` — a per-type grouping key: year / paper / section, undated rows ordered by slug NUMERICALLY so a rebuild cannot reshuffle the list · `mockKindNote`, the one-sentence 'what this is' line every type gets on its instructions page, INCLUDING past papers, so absence of a note never silently means 'the real thing'; TDD `tests/mock-catalogue.test.ts`). `MockCatalogueList` now takes PRE-COMPUTED groups — grouping is the caller's job so the rules stay unit-testable. Always-visible **Mocks** nav tab (ungated 2026-08-21 — `hasMocks` now drives only the catalogue's own exam rail, `mocksNav.ts`). Built by `scripts/mocks/build.ts` (+ `cdsSittings.ts`, the pure CDS sitting derivation; `mhtcetSittings.ts`, the MHT-CET registry — 45 bank labels but only 42 real sittings (three papers were uploaded twice and are MERGED via `mergeWith`), half derived from `scripts/mhtcet/config.ts` and half hand-written, carrying a per-paper `hold` for the 20 of 84 papers that cannot reconstruct whole; and `fingerprint.ts` + `baseline/` — the 'shipped mocks unchanged' acceptance check for any change to the shared build path). `lib/mocks/marking.ts` derives the instructions-screen marking copy, because MHT-CET is the first zero-penalty exam and the hard-coded copy asserted negative marking and told students to skip. **`lib/mocks/answers.ts`** (2026-09-07) is the ANSWER axis — `MockAnswerKey`/`SavedResponse` discriminated unions + `matchesNumericAnswer`/`isAnswered`/`verdictFor` — added for JEE Mains Section B, whose numeric-answer (NAT) questions carry no options and key off `questions.numeric_answer` (migration 0087 adds `attempt_answers.numeric_response`); `verdictFor` is the single source of right/wrong, shared by `gradeMock` and the review page so a score and its review cannot disagree. `scripts/mocks/jeeSittings.ts` is the JEE registry, DERIVED from `scripts/jee/papers/*.json` — a third discovery rule, because a 2025 JEE source file holds a whole DATE (two 75-question shifts), so a sitting is a `source_row` BLOCK within a file. **Share loop (2026-09-18, migration 0109):** the result screen carries `ShareResult` — a 'Pass this paper on' card offering WhatsApp (`wa.me` with no recipient, so WhatsApp opens its own group chooser) · the OS sheet via `navigator.share` (feature-detected after mount, never in the render body) · copy-link. What is shared is `/mock/<slug>`, NOT the result page (per-attempt, auth-gated, noindex — a recipient would just be redirected), and the SCORE IS OPT-IN, off by default: a score-forward share only fires for students who did well, and broadcasting scores into a class group rebuilds the peer ranking the engagement gate forbids leaderboards for. Pure core `lib/mocks/share.ts` (`buildMockShareUrl`/`buildMockShareText`/`buildWhatsappHref`, TDD `tests/mock-share.test.ts`; the canonical host is hardcoded, unlike `ShareMock`'s `window.location.origin`, so a preview deploy's URL can't escape into a group). Every link carries `utm_source=<channel>&utm_medium=share&utm_campaign=mock-result`, which needs no new attribution code — `parseAcquisition` (0106) already reads the triple, and it is not optional because **WhatsApp strips the referrer**. The channel is named honestly: only the explicit button claims `whatsapp`; the OS sheet is `share`, since it never reports the chosen app. Outbound taps go to `POST /api/mock/share` → `lib/mocks/shareLog.ts` → `share_events` (service-role; RLS on with zero policies, the `export_events`/`rate_limits` posture) — a TABLE and not a `user_activity` kind because that log is a learning-anchored allowlist and a share is distribution. It records INTENT, never delivery. `/mock/[slug]/opengraph-image.tsx` is the link-preview card the route never had (the quiz card's shape; anon client + `getMockCardBySlug`, the listing-columns read that leaves the question snapshot in the database). **Findings card (2026-09-18):** `result/Findings.tsx` renders the per-attempt diagnosis ABOVE the review list — easy marks dropped · easy ones looked at and left · pacing · the pooled subtopic picks · a link to `/performance` — from `lib/email/mockReport.buildMockReport`, the SAME core the report email uses, so the two surfaces cannot drift (its grain split is load-bearing: per-question facts from this attempt, subtopic picks POOLED across every paper). Headings match the email's wording; what the card adds is links — `#q<position>` anchors into the review cards below (`AttemptReviewList` carries the ids) and `goPracticeHref` per subtopic. **It passes a REAL `PeerMap` since 2026-09-24 (C4):** `loadFindings` reads `question_item_stats` via `readPeerAccuracy` on the SERVICE-ROLE client, scoped to this attempt's question ids — the one service-role read on a student page, decided deliberately — so an easy miss can say "62% of students got this right" and `rankWrong` ranks by recoverability. Question level only; nothing compares one student to another. Best-effort — a failed `getOwnPerformance` logs and renders nothing rather than costing the student their result. **`AttemptsList` sets `prefetch={false}` because of this card** (it now runs the 2026-09-15 RPC shape and that list renders one link per attempt on `/me`), pinned by `tests/mock-result-no-prefetch.test.ts`. Auth-gated `ƒ`, so the build never renders it: `npm run findings:smoke` (`scripts/mocks/findings-smoke.ts`) drives the data chain over real attempts and proves neither layout nor the RLS path. **Expiry sweep (2026-09-19):** `scripts/mocks/sweep-expired.ts` (`npm run mock:sweep`, DRY RUN by default) grades attempts whose timer ran out unattended, via the SAME `submitAttempt` the runner uses rather than a second grader. It exists because `submitAttempt(id,"expired")` had exactly one caller — `MockRunner.tsx`, client-side, with the tab open — so a closed laptop stranded the attempt in `in_progress` permanently: 118 of them, 1,285 answered questions, 84 students, oldest 2026-07-10, still accruing. Pure core `lib/mocks/sweep.ts` (`planSweep` — total by construction, every candidate lands in `sweep` or `skipped` so a row cannot be dropped in silence; skips rather than guesses when `expires_at` is unusable; clamps a stamp that would precede the attempt's own start; TDD `tests/mock-sweep.test.ts`). **`submitAttempt` gained an optional `at`**, defaulting to now so every live caller is unchanged: the sweep passes the attempt's own `expires_at`, because `readReportCandidates` filters on `submitted_at` and stamping `now` would have mailed 82 students a report about a July mock, while `buildActivityRow`'s explicit `created_at` is what the /drill ladder sorts on. `updated_at` deliberately stays real-now — a claim about when the student stopped and a record of what we did are different columns, and backdating both erased the sweep's own audit trail on the first pass. Runs hourly (`.github/workflows/sweep-expired.yml`), where `--apply` lives so that running the command by hand can never grade a hundred students' papers as a side effect; idempotent via the `status='in_progress'` guard. **`answer_correct` gets its mock emitter (2026-09-19), and it is CONDITIONAL BY DESIGN.** `lib/mocks/correctEvents.ts` (`selectAnswerCorrectEvents`/`answerCorrectDedupeKey`, TDD `tests/mock-correct-events.test.ts`) records a correct mock answer ONLY where that student already has an `answer_wrong` row for the question. The symmetric version was measured and rejected: **20,250 rows to change the drill pool by 484 questions (4.7% of the 10,395 ever missed)**, because students rarely meet the same question twice — the targeted rule gets the identical outcome in 537. Consequence to carry: a count of `answer_correct` is NOT "questions answered correctly" but RECOVERIES, whose only honest denominator is `answer_wrong`; the shape label is "Question recovered" and `pmf/snapshot.ts` says so. **GRACE is the trap** — `verdictFor` returns 1 for a grace question whether or not the student answered, and two recoveries RETIRE a question, so a naive `verdict === 1` rule would retire questions nobody attempted. The same blindness rules out deriving correctness by joining `attempt_answers` to `options.is_correct` (also blind to JEE Section-B numeric): that SQL predicts 559 rows where the real grader gives 537, and the 22-row gap IS the error. Hence **`regradeAttempt`** in `service.ts`, a read-only replay of the live `getMockById → loadAnswerKey → loadSavedAnswers → gradeMock` chain. Backfill `scripts/activity/backfill-answer-correct.ts` (`npm run activity:backfill-correct`, dry run by default) walks attempts OLDEST FIRST, since "previously missed" means as of that attempt — otherwise a September mistake justifies a July recovery; rows are backdated to `submitted_at` and keyed `answer_correct:<attemptId>:<questionId>`, the SAME key the live emitter writes, so a re-run after deploy cannot double-write (proven: a second --apply left 537 rows, 0 duplicates). **Chapter tests (2026-09-30):** `lib/mocks/sectional.ts` (size · duration · one-section blueprint · deterministic selection, TDD `tests/mock-sectional.test.ts`) + `scripts/mocks/build-sectional.ts` (`--plan` commits ids to `data/mht-cet-sectional.json`, then build) — the first `scope='sectional'` rows; every builder now writes through `lib/mocks/row.ts` `mockTestRow` (TDD `tests/mock-row.test.ts`). See MOCKS.md. **Past-paper downloads (2026-10-07, migration 0136):** a whole past paper (`source='pyq'`, `scope='full'`) downloads as its question paper + answer key via `_components/PaperDownload.tsx` (client box: asks `GET /api/export/access` on open and again after sign-in or payment, because it sits on CACHED pages where `router.refresh()` re-serves the same copy; reuses `PassOffer` with `onSignedIn` + checkout surface `paper_download`). Three entry points: under Start test on `/mock/[slug]`, the hub's "Download past papers" link, and the new `/mock/exam/[examSlug]/download` (ISR, indexable, past papers grouped by year, in the sitemap via `mockDownloadHref`; cache safety pinned by `tests/mock-download-page.test.ts`). `lib/mocks/paperExport.ts` turns a mock into ordered ids + a section label per question (TDD `tests/mock-paper-export.test.ts`); `lib/mocks/passLine.ts` is the locked card's sentence, which on a past paper also sells the download. See [[mock-tests]] + [[project-student-mobile-capture]].
│   ├── books/                              **superadmin-only** assembled PYQ books, for review before export (2026-08-31). `/books` (shelf) → `/books/[bookSlug]` (TOC with live per-exam counts) → `/books/[bookSlug]/[chapterSlug]` → `BookChapterReader` (client). Every page is gated by `getSessionSuperadmin()` — that is the boundary; the nav tab is only chrome, drawn from the `isSuperadmin` flag ALREADY on `HeaderSession`, so no new endpoint and no server session read (AppHeader stays a cacheable shell — see [[shell-component-decaches-site]]). A book stores NO content: it is a derived view over questions that already exist, so a fix made anywhere shows up here. A chapter renders as **"NDA PYQ" → the NDA half → "CDS PYQ" → the CDS half**, each oldest-first. The reader's unit is the SET, not the question — 3,175 of the 3,180 questions carry a shared `context`, and in Reading Comprehension one passage feeds 5+ questions, so a set is rendered whole with its passage printed once above it. Sets are collapsed by default (Vocabulary is 773 questions) with Expand-all; answers show by DEFAULT, since this is a staff review surface with nothing to gate. **Multi-book since 2026-08-31 (second)**: `lib/books/registry.ts` declares each book as data (`BookDefinition` — slug/title/subject/`sections[]`/chapters, `bookExams()`), `lib/books/order.ts` carries per-EXAM sitting rules (`EXAM_SITTING`: NDA by `pyq_month`, CDS by source-filename roman) + opt-in per-chapter `groupSubtopics` (Layout A — one Directions line per homogeneous subtopic block; legal ONLY where 0 sets span a subtopic, measured per chapter). `lib/books/`: `order` (sections/blocks/sittings) · `query` (loadBookMeta/loadBookChapter; 1000-row `.range()` paging + 200-id `.in()` chunking) · `sync` (add-only membership plan) · `curate` (planSetMove/planSetToSection — moves SETS, never questions) · `service` (server actions, re-derive server-side, `requireSuperadmin()` each) · `print` (`numberChapter` — ONE pass yields paper numbers + answer-key rows in rendered block order; `chapterContents` — the front-matter contents table, derived from that SAME numbering so contents, paper and key cannot disagree). A `groupSubtopics` entry may carry **`members: string[]`** so ONE printed heading covers SEVERAL bank subtopics (Grammar merges its three fill-a-blank subtopics — see the 2026-08-31 (fourth) Decisions entry); the block then filters the ORDERED set list rather than concatenating per member, or a merged block would silently lose the half's chronology. **Print view** `/books/[bookSlug]/[chapterSlug]/print` (+ `_print/BookChapterPrint.tsx` + `printStyles.ts`): the chapter as US-Letter 2-column pages via the browser's own print engine (`@page` letter, Cambria 10pt, `break-inside: avoid` on questions never sets, answers-only key at chapter end on a fresh page), opening with a full-width **contents table** giving each subtopic's range under BOTH exams (a dash = that exam does not ask it; a flat chapter gets section ranges only, since a per-subtopic range there would be non-contiguous and false) and repeating each range on its block heading; print-to-PDF is the share path until the docx script exists — **and the contents table lives in the PREVIEW ONLY, so a docx export must call `chapterContents`/`numberChapter` rather than reimplement them.** Smoke `scripts/books/smoke.ts` (block accounting: every set/question in exactly one block; contents covers every printed question exactly once) · curation round-trip `scripts/books/verify-curation.ts` · **`scripts/books/subtopic-report.ts`** (re-measures the Layout-A gate per chapter — spanning sets, questions-under-a-wrong-heading, passage-vs-directions-line, `--spanning`/`--directions` detail; counts are against BLOCKS so a merged chapter reads clean) · **`scripts/books/render-print.tsx`** (renders the print view to `generated-papers/*.html` with no server or login — every /books route is auth-gated `force-dynamic`, so `next build` proves only that they compile; run via `--tsconfig scripts/books/tsconfig.render.json`, which supplies the automatic JSX runtime) · tests `tests/books-{order,sync,curate,print,registry}.test.ts`.
│   ├── formula/                           **questions by the identity their SOLUTION uses** (2026-09-17) — a third addressing axis over the same bank, next to `/browse` (taxonomy) and `/questions/*` (its cacheable per-chapter counterpart). `/formula` (index, ordered by WEIGHT rather than teaching order — the point of the page is which identities the chapter leans on) → `/formula/[slug]`. Static + ISR, cookie-free anon client. Each page renders the identity, its symbol legend, stats, an **Used alongside** row derived from real co-occurrence in the same solutions (not a similarity guess), then every question whose solution invokes it, easiest-first. `FilteredQuestions.tsx` is a CLIENT island carrying the exam (NDA · MHT-CET · MH State Board · CBSE) and PYQ/Practice filters; a filter row renders only where it would do something, and counts come from the FULL set so they don't move as you narrow. Filters are deliberately NOT in the URL — `useSearchParams()` bails the prerender and a same-route query-param link is prefetched with no `loading.tsx` boundary (the 2026-09-15 outage shape); cost is that a filtered view isn't shareable. Membership is hand-classified: see `scripts/formula/README.md`.
│   ├── quiz/                              public lead-magnet quiz funnel (migrations 0033/0034; 2026-06-09). `/quiz/[slug]` = the taker (`QuizTaker.tsx`, client) + `opengraph-image.tsx`; **LIVE, not dormant** — 4 quizzes published, 16 leads. The reveal gate is three-way (`resolveQuizGate`, `lib/quiz/gate.ts`): a SIGNED-IN student skips it entirely, an anon returner gets one-tap sticky consent, a first-timer the name+mobile+consent form. **2026-09-18 — the signed-in branch stopped being a dead end.** It used to grade and return, writing nothing anywhere: the score lived in React state and died on refresh, while `quiz_taken` sat in ACTIVITY_KINDS and the 0052 CHECK with a label in three surfaces and ZERO emitters (fourth instance of the 0107/0108 class). Now `POST /api/public-quiz/submit` emits it through the RLS-bound client — pure core `lib/quiz/activity.ts` (`buildQuizTakenEvent` refs the PUBLIC SLUG, not the uuid, since `quizzes` is admin-RLS and a student page could not resolve one; `parseQuizAttempt` degrades rather than throws, because `metadata` is schemaless jsonb). **`/quiz/attempts`** is the reader (auth-gated `ƒ`, noindex, mirrors `/mock/attempts`) over `lib/quiz/attempts.ts` — own-row `user_activity` via the 0052 select policy, so no new table, policy or migration. SCORES ONLY, deliberately: an authed taker's answers are never stored and an analytics log is the wrong home for them. Probed by `npm run quiz:smoke-attempts`. The gate also gained a **sign-in door** (5 of 13 lead mobiles already had accounts), which needs `lib/quiz/resume.ts` — answers are React state and `qb:lead:v1` holds the IDENTITY, not the attempt, so /login would have destroyed the quiz; the sessionStorage blob is re-read as untrusted input. `lib/quiz/share.ts` tags the results share (`utm_campaign=quiz-result`, canonical host): untagged + WhatsApp's stripped referrer made `parseAcquisition` file every arrival as `source: "direct"` — misattributed, not merely unmeasured. See [[project-public-quiz-funnel]].
│   ├── dashboard/                         page.tsx + loading.tsx — stat cards, by-exam bars, recent uploads (admin). Redirects TEACHER → /browse; **any org-less visitor (anon or self-serve student) → /browse** (the old "Account not linked" card was dropped 2026-06-01). Admin Quick-Actions grid (lg:grid-cols-4): Browse · Upload · **Question reports** (live red open-count badge via `count:"exact"` on `question_reports`) · **Concept reports** (same badge pattern on `concept_reports`, 2026-06-02) · **Members** · **Access** (links to /dashboard/entitlements, 2026-06-01).
│   ├── dashboard/students/[id]/          per-student surfaces, superadmin-only, both `force-dynamic` + noindex. `page.tsx` = Profile (identity · captured profile · contactability · engagement · mock summary · activity); `performance/page.tsx` = the diagnosis (stat row + intake disclosure · **exam row then subject row** · coverage · **time analysis** · accuracy by difficulty · Where-to-focus · chapter accordion · wrong + skipped audits · projected score), at `max-w-5xl` against the profile's `max-w-3xl`. **The nav is TWO rows, not one (2026-09-15)**: lanes are (exam, subject) and sorted by answered-count, so on a multi-exam student one row INTERLEAVES the exams (NDA·English … CDS·Chemistry … NDA·History, 18 pills), and the exam row appears only when the student has sat more than one — 112 of 124 have sat exactly one. Selection lives in `lib/performance/laneNav.ts`, not here. **The four list components MOVED to `src/components/performance/` on 2026-09-18** when `/performance` began sharing them, and the page body moved with them into `PerformanceBody.tsx` — this route is now the staff SHELL (AppHeader + StudentTabs + the fetch) around that shared body. `ChapterAccordion.tsx`/`AuditList.tsx`/`ProjectionList.tsx`/`Pager.tsx` are CLIENT components because all four lists PAGE (`PERF_PAGE_SIZE` = 5, `lib/paging.ts`); `ProjectionList` also carries the **Chapters | Subtopics** toggle (migration 0100), which resets to page 1 on switch because page 4 of 7 chapters means nothing among 111 subtopics: the whole payload is already computed server-side, so a URL param would re-run a 1 MB RPC to show five rows it already had — the cost, stated, is that a page position is not shareable. The pager is prev/position/next rather than numbered buttons because the largest skipped audit in production is 106 rows = 22 pages. `TimeCard` is server-rendered (no state). A SEPARATE ROUTE, not more sections: the profile is a field list, the diagnosis needs the width and loads a far heavier aggregate that opening a profile should not pay for. `StudentTabs.tsx` is shared by both so the header, back-link and active tab cannot drift. **`attempt/[attemptId]/page.tsx` fixes a live defect**: `/mock/attempt/[id]/result` is own-row by construction (`.eq(user_id)` + own-row RLS), so every attempt row the admin dashboard rendered 404'd and bounced the superadmin to `/mock`. This route reads via `getAttemptReviewForAdmin` (service-role) and reuses `mock/_components/AttemptReviewList.tsx`, extracted from the student result page so there is ONE renderer with two callers — fixing one and not the other is the failure this repo has hit twice. `AttemptsList` gained an optional `reviewBase`; in-progress rows never repoint, because only the owner may resume. **All three list surfaces DRILL DOWN (2026-09-15)**: both audits carry an Open button (the skipped one could not before, because the core collected `wrongIds` and not `seenBlankIds`), the accordion links the wrong/skipped COUNTS themselves rather than adding a sixth element to an already-dense row — without it, opening the three wrong answers in a subtopic meant hunting that subtopic through 14 pages of the audit below — and the projection links each row NAME to the topic in the bank rather than to the student's own mistakes. Which link goes where is decided in `lib/performance/links.ts`, not here. **Every `<Link>` on this whole surface sets `prefetch={false}` (2026-09-15, after a production outage)** — these pills are query-param links on the SAME route, so a prefetch has no loading boundary to stop at and is a full page render of a ~500 ms / 1.27 MB RPC; the roster renders one per student, 315 of them. Pinned by `tests/dashboard-students-no-prefetch.test.ts`, which COUNTS `<Link` against `prefetch={false}` per file rather than parsing tags — JSX attributes wrap across lines and contain `>` inside arrow functions, so a tag-matching regex is a probe that can quietly stop matching.
│   ├── dashboard/reports/                 admin triage page for `question_reports` (migration 0024). Server Component, admin-only via `getSessionMember` → 404 for non-admins. Status filter chips (All / Open / In review / Resolved / Won't fix / Duplicate) routed via `?status=...`. Per-report card: question breadcrumb + severity-coloured category chip + details quoted callout + Open-question link + `ReportRowActions` client island (status `<select>` → PATCH `/api/reports/[id]` → toast + router.refresh, resolution-note field visible on terminal statuses).
│   ├── dashboard/notes-reports/           admin triage page for `concept_reports` (migration 0028 / 2026-06-02). Parallel to /dashboard/reports: Server Component, admin-only, same status filter chips. Per-report card: exam·subject·chapter·subtopic breadcrumb + concept name + severity-coloured category chip + details callout + **"Open concept →"** link to the live anchor `/notes/{subject_route}/{chapter_slug}/{subtopic_slug}#{concept_slug}` + `ConceptReportRowActions` client island (PATCH `/api/notes/reports/[id]`).
│   ├── dashboard/members/                 admin member-management UI (2026-05-26): page.tsx (Server Component, admin-only — TEACHER redirected to /browse) hands an initial `MemberRow[]` list to `MembersClient.tsx`. Add form: name (required) + email (required, becomes username) + password (≥8 chars) + role dropdown (TEACHER default, ADMIN). Per-row: role `<Select>` (disabled for self), `Reset password` (opens dialog with new-password input), `Remove` (opens confirm dialog; auth user kept, only `org_members` row deleted). Last-admin protect + self-protect enforced server-side. All actions POST to /api/admin/members.
│   ├── dashboard/pricing/                 superadmin pass catalogue (2026-09-27): page.tsx (force-dynamic, getSessionSuperadmin → /browse) hands `Plan[]` + `PaywallSettings` to `PricingAdminClient.tsx` — list with on/off-sale toggle, edit/new dialog (id + url_key fixed after creation; price in ₹, days blank = lifetime, scope from SELLABLE_SCOPES, perks one per line), and the free-mock limit card (checkbox + number). All actions POST /api/admin/plans.
│   ├── superadmin/orgs/ + orgs/[id]/       **organisations, on their own pages (2026-10-06)**, moved out of the first `/superadmin` tab (which keeps the two queues and links here; `lib/superadmin/tabs.ts` is now teachers + contact). `/superadmin/orgs` lists every org (members · admins · batches · papers · questions, a "No admin" badge) with New organization; each row opens `/superadmin/orgs/[id]` (`OrgDetailClient`): rename · counts · members with a native role `<select>` (`setMemberRoleAction` → `updateMemberRole`, last-admin guard) · `AddMemberDialog` · a delete danger zone. `lib/superadmin/admin.ts` `renameOrg` / `getOrgDetail` / `deleteOrg` / `countOrgs`; delete needs the exact name typed and is refused for a protected org, an org owning questions (it would cascade them) or one with reports (their FKs RESTRICT). **Migration 0135 `deletion_protected`** + trigger `private.organizations_protect` refuses deleting OR renaming a flagged org whatever the caller; LWS Pune is flagged (prod + test seed) because it owns the bank and `/api/sync/mock` finds it BY NAME. A flag, not "refuse while it owns questions", because 40 integration tests delete fixture orgs that own fixture questions. Spec `tests/superadmin-orgs.test.ts`. UserMenu carries an Organisations link for the superadmin.
│   ├── dashboard/entitlements/            admin comp-access UI (2026-06-01): page.tsx (Server Component, admin-only — TEACHER → /browse) hands an `EntitlementAdminRow[]` to `EntitlementsClient.tsx`. Grant form: student email + scope (default 'all') + optional expiry date + note → POST grant (require-signup-first). Per-row: status/source badges + Revoke (confirm dialog → status='revoked', kept for audit). All actions POST to /api/admin/entitlements.
│   ├── dashboard/papers/                  collaborative paper builder (migration 0039, 2026-06-15) — ANY org member (ADMIN **or** TEACHER; unlike the other dashboard pages it does NOT redirect TEACHER). `page.tsx` → `PapersListClient` (new/delete papers); `[id]/page.tsx` → `PaperEditor` (section progress bars + per-section assignee chips + questions grouped by section, **each rendered by `/browse`'s `QuestionCard` with `hideCart`** — KaTeX stem + options + answer reveal — with a control bar beneath carrying remove + move-to-section + **up/down reorder** + "added by" + the usage chip; editable section template dialog incl. **assignee toggle chips** + finalize/reopen + `PaperDownload` reusing `/api/export`; loads full `QuestionRow`s via `queryQuestionsByIds` (NOT the slim `QuestionPreview` — it has no options), `canEdit` = `getSessionSuperadmin()` per 0056; org-member names come from the service-role `listMembers` since `org_members` read RLS is admin-only) + `AddQuestionsPanel` (**a `Sheet` opened from the header "Add questions" button** — it used to be a permanent 50%-width column, which was what forced the question text into a 2-line clamp; bank search via `queryQuestions`, auto-files by subject, exam filter seeded from `dominantExamId`, rows rendered through `KatexRenderer`) + a "Browse the bank" link to `/browse` (collect in the cart there, then commit via the cart's "Add to paper" — still the bulk path, with the full filter set). Server actions in `dashboard/papers/actions.ts` (authed cookie client — RLS is the boundary; any org member is an editor); `listActivePapersAction` + `addCartToPaperAction` feed the cart-commit path. Reached via the UserMenu "Papers" link (org members only). See [[collaborative-paper-builder]].
│   ├── dashboard/activity/                engagement usage-shape readout (migration 0053, 2026-07-12) — Server Component, admin-only (`getSessionMember` → /browse for non-admin). Calls `getActivityShape` (service-role `get_activity_shape` RPC), runs the pure `classifyUsageShape`, and renders a verdict banner (daily/burst/mixed/insufficient = the streak-vs-goal-progress call) + by-kind table + gap-distribution bars + recency + daily-active bars. The "measure before building a mechanic" surface — see the **Engagement engine** gate in CLAUDE.md + [[project-engagement-engine]].
│   ├── components/analytics/ClarityScript.tsx + lib/analytics/clarity.ts   **Microsoft Clarity recordings + heatmaps (2026-09-30)** — the qualitative layer over the funnel (WHY a student stops; Vercel/funnelEvents/user_activity only count WHAT). Pathname-gated client island in the root layout: `shouldLoadClarity` refuses every staff prefix (`/dashboard` `/superadmin` `/account` `/upload(s)` `/papers` `/books` — where rosters, lead mobiles and `organizations.name` render) on a SEGMENT boundary, and the island issues `clarity("stop"/"start")` on soft navigation across that line since the loaded tag is global. Reads no server API (caching untouched). OFF without `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLARITY_PROJECT_ID` (build-time; only a `[a-z0-9]+` token is accepted because it is interpolated into an inline script). `data-clarity-mask` on the mock score, `/me` email and the quiz "Continue as" line; set the project's masking to Strict in Clarity. Named on `/privacy`. Spec `tests/analytics-clarity.test.ts`.
│   ├── components/chat/ChatWidget.tsx + lib/chat/ + api/chat/{faq,open}/ + dashboard/chat/   **V, the floating FAQ helper (2026-09-29)** — a bottom-right mascot on every page (root layout; no server read at mount, so caching is untouched), answering 6 predefined STUDENT questions as templates over live facts (`lib/chat/faq.ts` over `EXAM_REGISTRY` + `listActivePlans` + a PUBLIC count — never hardcoded numbers). No LLM call. Usage lands in `chat_interactions` (migration 0127, RLS on + zero policies, service-role only): one `launcher_open` per page mount via `/api/chat/open`, one `faq_click` per chip inside `/api/chat/faq`; read at `/dashboard/chat` (superadmin). The same two moments also ping Vercel (`chat_launcher_click`/`chat_faq_click`). **Dormant on purpose:** `/api/chat` + `lib/chat/{anthropic,validate}.ts` + `buildSystemPrompt` are a working Claude Haiku free-text path (raw fetch, no SDK), unwired from the widget until usage justifies an `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`. Mascot art: `public/chat/v-{idle,laugh,think,talk}.png`.
│   ├── upload/                            admin-only: Stepper → Dropzone (file pick) → preview summary bar → animated success
│   ├── browse/                            page.tsx + loading.tsx, composed from:
│   │       + **BrowseLanding (2026-08-13)** — what the BARE page renders instead of 25 questions, for an anonymous viewer with zero filters (48% of all /browse renders, measured). Exam start-pills + the 8 densest chapters; **every link applies a filter IN PLACE** (`/browse?examId=…`), never navigating to `/` or `/questions` — deliberately not a third directory page, since `/` already owns exam cards and `/questions` the chapter crawl hub. Skips `queryQuestions` entirely (that unfiltered shape ran ~2.1 s and produced 98% of the DB's statement timeouts); data comes from three already-cached reads via `loadLandingPanel` in page.tsx. Org members keep the live list — their reads include own-org PRIVATE rows. Pure core in `lib/questions/browseLanding.ts`; pill counts from `lib/questions/defaultViewCounts.ts`.
│   │       + Hero (unfiltered /browse only) — compact: headline (the page `<h1>`) + one-line sub + a 2-stat band (`{total} public questions` · `Free`). Exam pill rows ("Available now / Coming soon") removed 2026-06-01 (redundant with the sidebar exam filter + the then-current header exam pill, itself removed 2026-08-21); guides + notes discoverability lives in PrimaryNav + Footer + `/nda`.
│   │       + BackToNotes — floating "← Back to notes" pill, bottom-LEFT (opposite CartPill). Captures `?from=`/`?fromLabel=` from a /notes or /guide drill link into sessionStorage (key `qb:returnTo:v1`), strips the params from the URL, and restores from storage on later pages so the pill survives pagination + filtering. Dismissable. Client-only (reads window + sessionStorage, like CartPill).
│   │       + FilterBar — sections rendered in a `mode`-driven order: `live` for desktop sidebar with current-order, `staged` for mobile sheet with difficulty + PYQ year hoisted above chapter accordions; PYQ presets Last 3 / Last 5 / All years backed by `selectRecentYears`; chapter and subtopic rows render as `Name (count)` sorted by q-count descending, zero-count entries hidden, counts context-aware (shrink when other filters narrow the bank).
│   │       + MobileFilters — Sheet owns a staged Filters buffer + sticky bottom bar with Reset / Show results; cascading fields auto-commit to refresh option lists while non-cascading fields stage until Apply.
│   │       + FitCoverageNote.tsx — amber inline caveat under the chips when the Syllabus-fit filter is on but the visible chapters are not all screened (`fitCoverage` → partial/unscoped). Renders nothing when the filter is off or coverage is full.
│   │       + ActiveFilterChips — horizontal-scrolls on mobile, no-wrap; chapter/subtopic chips render as "Chapter: Name (count)"; right edge has a Copy-link chip backed by `buildShareUrl`.
│   │       + QuestionList — groups consecutive set-siblings under a passage banner via groupBySet; takes `includeExam` to surface the exam in each card's breadcrumb when no exam filter is active; threads `isLoggedIn` + `resourceTags: Map<questionId, ResourceTags>` from a batched `getResourceTagsForQuestions` fetch.
│   │       + QuestionCard — expand button and cart-toggle are sibling buttons (never nested); breadcrumb built via `buildBreadcrumb` helper; Q-badge hidden on mobile with `#N` inline; expanded body has `overflow-x-auto [&_.katex]:max-w-full` so wide math scrolls within the card; ZoomableImage dialog has an explicit round close button overlay.
│   │            · **Backlink chip row** between header and expand panel — chapter→playbook + subtopic→notes chips via `getQuestionResources`, overridden by principle / concept tag-specific chips when DB tags exist.
│   │            · **Click-to-reveal answer** — every viewer (incl. ADMIN) sees plain options + "Tap an option to check your answer" hint; clicking an option reveals correctness (green border + "Correct" on right answer; red border + "Your pick" on wrong pick). Picked state resets on card collapse. Admins who need to verify answers without clicking go to `/questions/[id]/edit`. (The 2026-05-18 `isAdmin` short-circuit was reversed 2026-05-25 — see Decisions log.)
│   │            · **Metered answer-reveal (2026-07-12)** — anon get **3 free** reveals (a shared localStorage budget across /browse + /board via `useRevealMeter`, pure `revealDecision`); option-pick + show-solution both go through `tryReveal`, and once the budget's spent the answer is replaced by `<RevealSignInPrompt>` (client `@/components/reveal/`). Signed-in = unlimited. Client-side so pages stay ISR/SEO-clean; the gated content is public PYQs anyway (a nudge, not a boundary — see [[signup-gate-placement]]).
│   │            · **BookmarkButton** in the header row (beside `CartToggle`) — anon → sign-in nudge (`?next` back), signed-in → optimistic save toggle via the app-wide `useBookmarks` (`question_bookmarks`, migration 0047). Surfaced on the `/saved` page.
│   │            · **ReportQuestionDialog** at the bottom of the expanded view — Radix Dialog; logged-in form (category + details) POSTs to `/api/questions/[id]/reports`; anon gets sign-in prompt + mailto fallback.
│   │            · **Footer row (expanded card)** — `Edit question` link on the left (rendered when `canEdit` — ADMIN OR TEACHER as of 2026-05-26; gate widened from `isAdmin`, the page-level prop was renamed to match) + `[Q# · disambiguator · year]` provenance bracket in the middle (composed by `formatProvenance`, NDA uses month / others use note) + `ReportQuestionDialog` on the right. `flex-wrap` on mobile when the bracket pushes a line.
│   │       + Pagination — compact `Prev / Page X of Y / Next` on mobile, numbered jumper on sm+; click triggers smooth scroll-to-top; client component.
│   │       + DownloadDialog — `max-h-[90dvh]` flex column: header · **`min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto`** body · non-sticky `shrink-0` footer (the `min-h-0` is load-bearing — without it the body won't shrink, the dialog outgrows its max-height, and the footer overlaps the last control; fixed 2026-07-28 with the footer's old `sticky bottom-0`, which caused the overlap, removed and the 4-button row set to `sm:flex-wrap` inside `sm:max-w-xl` so "Done" can't overflow the left edge). Options: document title · include-solutions · group-by-subtopic · **cite-the-source (2026-07-28, `includeSourceTag`)**. Download buttons (Question Paper + Answer Key + staff-only Tagged sheet), one HTTP request per file with `kind` selector. **Auth-aware (2026-07-11):** anon see a single "Sign in to download" CTA (`?next` back) in place of the buttons; students see paper+key; only org staff see the tagged sheet — all derived from the shared pure `resolveExportAccess` (`lib/export/access.ts`).
│   │       + PassOffer — the box's offer to a visitor without the pass (2026-10-04): the plan's perks + price, Google sign-in over the page (`renderGoogleButton`, redirect `GoogleSignInButton` as fallback), then `useCheckout`; `router.refresh()` moves the box to the download view
│   │       + CartPill — floating pill with pill-in entrance + count-pulse on add + Sheet panel (per-row remove, sort toggle Added-order/By-chapter, Clear via branded confirm dialog); bottom respects `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` for iOS home indicator; CartItem renders math via `KatexRenderer` with `safeSnippet`-truncated text. **Footer is two paper doors, no download (2026-09-11).** `isOrgMember` (signed-in ADMIN/TEACHER, from the page) gets **"Add to paper"** → `AddToPaperDialog` (pick an existing draft, bulk-commit via `addCartToPaperAction`, auto-filed by subject, idempotent — the collaborative-paper add path that replaced the URL-driven `/browse?paper=` per-card mode, Decisions log 2026-06-15) **plus "Create paper"** → `CreatePaperDialog` (new paper, then navigate into it). Everyone else gets the SAME "Create paper" button pointed at `/request-access` — `papers.org_id` is NOT NULL and every papers RLS policy scopes to the caller's org (0039), so a student cannot own one. **The cart's Download button was removed in the same change**: downloads are teacher-gated, so for non-staff it was only ever the request-access funnel, and staff still reach the cart export via the top-of-page `DownloadDialog`'s "Selected · N" mode (`cartAvailable`) or `PaperDownload` on the paper page. Props narrowed to `{ isOrgMember }` — `filters`/`isSignedIn` existed solely for the removed dialog.
│   │       + CreatePaperDialog — cart → BRAND-NEW paper (2026-09-11). One title field, then `createPaperWithQuestions` → `router.push('/dashboard/papers/<id>')`. Sibling of `AddToPaperDialog`, which targets an EXISTING draft and stays on /browse; that asymmetry is deliberate (button = take me there, dialog = I'm still collecting). Org-member only — the footer routes everyone else to /request-access rather than opening a dialog that would fail at `requireMember()`. Clears the cart and closes the Sheet ONLY on success; a mid-sequence failure leaves an empty paper, says so, and keeps the selection so "Add to paper" can retry into it.
│   │       + createPaperWithQuestions.ts — the `createPaperAction` → `addCartToPaperAction` sequence BOTH cart doors run (the footer button, and `AddToPaperDialog`'s create mode, which is kept because it auto-flips on for an org with no drafts yet). One copy so the two cannot drift; returns `createdId` on a post-create failure so the caller can report the empty paper instead of denying it exists.
│   ├── questions/page.tsx                 **Crawl hub for the landing pages (2026-07-29)** — every chapter grouped exam → subject, with question counts. `revalidate = 86400`. Exists so the 317 landing pages are internally linked rather than sitemap-only orphans (internal links are what distribute ranking signal), and so a student can go exam → chapter in two clicks.
│   ├── questions/[examSlug]/[subjectSlug]/[chapterSlug]/page.tsx   **Public per-chapter question landing page (2026-07-29)** — the cacheable, indexable counterpart to `/browse`: same questions, addressed by PATH instead of query string, which is what lets Next cache it (a page reading `searchParams` never can) and what gives Google 317 real pages to rank instead of one `/browse` URL for ~24k questions. Read-only by design (no filters, no download dialog, PUBLIC rows only via the anon client). Renders up to 50 questions through the shared `QuestionList` — **wrapped in `<Suspense>`** because its `BookmarkButton` calls `useSearchParams()`, which otherwise bails the whole static prerender to client rendering. Also: breadcrumb, `/browse` deep link with the chapter pre-filtered, notes/guide backlinks via the shared `getQuestionResources`, and sibling-chapter links (the crawl path between landings). `revalidate = 86400`; `generateStaticParams` pre-builds the 40 busiest inside a try/catch so a Supabase blip can't fail a deploy. Data + slug logic in `lib/questions/{landing,slugs}.ts`. See [[public-landing-pages]].
│   ├── llms.txt/route.ts                  **`/llms.txt` (2026-09-29)** — the llmstxt.org index for language-model crawlers, GENERATED from the registry + cached catalogue by the pure `lib/seo/llmsTxt.ts` (TDD `tests/llms-txt.test.ts`): description with the live total, the section hubs, one line per exam home with its count (`noPublicContent` and zero-count exams left out). Unconfirmed as a signal; cheap and cannot rot. `robots.ts` also names `OAI-SearchBot` + `ChatGPT-User` as explicit groups carrying the wildcard's disallows (a named group REPLACES the wildcard — asserted rule-for-rule by `tests/robots-rules.test.ts`); GPTBot deliberately unnamed. `layout.tsx` emits the site-wide Organization + WebSite JSON-LD from `lib/seo/siteJsonLd.ts` (`tests/site-jsonld.test.ts`; genuine `sameAs` only, no logo — there is no logo file); profile constants live in `lib/brand.ts`.
│   ├── exams/[examSlug]/page.tsx          **Per-exam home page, built from data (2026-09-29)** — one for every registry exam with public content; `/exams/nda` redirects to the hand-built `/nda`, which stays. Shaped by the pure `buildExamHome` (`lib/exam/examHome.ts`, TDD `tests/exam-home.test.ts`) from the registry entry, the chapter-landing index (subjects → chapters with counts, years rolled up from the 0126 profiles) and the catalogue head-count; links what each registry flag unlocks (mocks · notes if shipped · guides · board). Opens with the one-sentence `examHomeDescription`, which is also its `<meta>`. Static via `generateStaticParams`, `revalidate = 86400`, anon client only. Linked from every `/questions` exam heading and every notes chapter's "exam home" pill (`examHomeHref` — that pill was `/${examName.toLowerCase()}`, a dead link for every exam but NDA). In the sitemap per exam.
│   ├── dashboard/questions/[id]/edit/     Editor page — **moved here from `/questions/[id]/edit` on 2026-07-29** so the public landing pages could take that URL space (Next forbids two differently-named dynamic segments at the same position — `[id]` vs `[examSlug]`); a `next.config.mjs` redirect keeps old bookmarks working, and it now sits under the `/dashboard` middleware matcher + the existing robots disallow. (ADMIN + TEACHER as of migration 0025 / 2026-05-26): two-column, sticky save bar, Edit/Preview tab, dropzone-style image slots. Question diagram inline under the Context field; textareas (question + options A–D) accept clipboard image paste. Set-bound questions get a "Set · N questions" chip + propagation hint + "Remove from set" button (POST /api/questions/[id]/leave-set — editor, not admin). Save returns to the previous page (router.back()). **TEACHER-gated chrome:** the `Delete` button in the save bar and the entire `Visibility` section are hidden when `isAdmin === false`; the visibility change is also rejected server-side via `applyEdit`'s `forbidden_field` guard. Non-org-member redirected to /browse.
│   ├── uploads/page.tsx                   admin-only upload index (Server Component): paginated full list of upload_jobs for the org (20/page, newest first), reuses browse Pagination; `?page=` clamped beyond the last page. "View all" link on dashboard points here.
│   ├── uploads/[id]/                      admin-only upload-detail: page.tsx (server) lists questions linked via upload_job_id, with header card; UploadHeaderActions (whole-upload delete dialog), PyqMetadataControl (3-field bulk-set: Year select + Month + Comment with confirm-on-overwrite dialog), QuestionListItem (per-row Edit + Delete)
│   ├── api/auth/callback/                 Supabase OAuth code exchange
│   ├── api/upload/{preview,commit}/       two-stage admin upload (preview accepts optional examId — auto-detected from Excel "Course" column via detectCourse + resolveExam if blank — and pyqYear/pyqMonth/pyqNote on formData; runs propagateSetContext to fill Set siblings from the first row's context; stages everything on upload_jobs.staged_rows; commit passes through to commitStaged)
│   ├── api/questions/[id]/                PUT (editor — ADMIN or TEACHER via `requireEditor`) + DELETE (admin only via `requireAdmin`): edit text+options+taxonomy+image paths; delete cascades options + cleans storage objects. PUT also fans out a context change to every sibling sharing the same set_id (only context propagates; everything else stays row-local). PUT stamps `last_edited_by` + `last_edited_at` on the question via `applyEdit` (migration 0025), and threads `member.role` so `applyEdit` can reject TEACHER visibility changes with HTTP 403 (`forbidden_field`).
│   ├── api/questions/[id]/leave-set/      POST (editor — ADMIN or TEACHER via `requireEditor`): clears set_id on this row only. Detaches the question from its passage group; siblings untouched. Idempotent. Teachers can use this as a content correction (the screenshot's Q2-not-in-Set-1 case).
│   ├── api/questions/[id]/reports/        POST (JSON, signed-in user — anon → 401). Body: `{ category, details? }`. Validates category enum + details ≤ 2000 chars, calls `createReport` helper which resolves question.org_id and denormalizes onto the report row. Returns 201 + `{id}` on success, 409 on duplicate-open-report (partial unique index), 404 not-found, 422 invalid input.
│   ├── api/reports/[id]/                  PATCH (JSON, admin only via `requireAdmin`). Body: `{ status, resolutionNote? }`. Calls `updateReport` helper — RLS scopes to admin's own-org reports; auto-stamps `resolved_at` + `resolved_by` on terminal statuses (resolved / wont-fix / duplicate). Reverting to open / in-review clears resolution stamps.
│   ├── api/notes/reports/                 POST (JSON, signed-in user via `getSessionUser` — anon → 401; **NOT org-member gated** since concepts are global content). Body: `{ subtopicSlug, conceptSlug, category, details? }`. Calls `createConceptReport` — validates the slug pair against the shipped notes registry (tampered/unknown → 404) + resolves org_id from a PUBLIC question in the concept's subtopic. 201 `{id}` / 409 duplicate-open / 404 unknown-concept / 422 invalid.
│   ├── api/notes/reports/[id]/            PATCH (JSON, admin only via `requireAdmin`). Body: `{ status, resolutionNote? }`. Calls `updateConceptReport` (parallel to `updateReport`, table `concept_reports`).
│   ├── api/admin/members/                 POST (JSON, admin only via `requireAdmin`). Single endpoint with action discriminator: `{ action: "list" | "create" | "reset" | "remove" | "update_role", ... }`. Calls into `src/lib/members/admin.ts` helpers using the service-role admin client (auth.admin.* needs it; `org_members` reads gate on admin RLS anyway). Returns `{ ok, ...extras }` on success or `{ error }` on failure. Handles `already_member` / `email_taken_other_org` 409s, `would_remove_last_admin` / `cannot_remove_self` / `cannot_change_own_role` 403s, validator-rejection 400s.
│   ├── api/uploads/[id]/                  DELETE + PATCH (JSON, admin only). DELETE removes all questions linked via upload_job_id + their storage objects + the upload_jobs row. PATCH bulk-updates pyq_year/pyq_month/pyq_note across the upload's questions; partial fields honoured, explicit null clears.
│   ├── api/sync/mock/                     POST (JSON): receives a finalized mock from a sibling app (e.g. MHT_CET_AI). Bearer-token auth via SYNC_SHARED_SECRET. Idempotent + content-hash dedup + attempt-stats merging.
│   ├── api/admin/entitlements/            POST (JSON, admin only via requireAdmin). Action discriminator `list | grant | revoke` → src/lib/entitlements/admin.ts helpers (service-role). grant is require-signup-first (email → existing auth user, never auto-create); user_not_found → 404.
│   ├── api/activity/event/                POST (signed-in, rate-limited 120/h): the GENERIC client beacon beside api/activity/practice — `parseClientEvent` admits only a `surface_viewed` (known surface) or a `paywall_event` shown/checkout_dismissed; anything graded or server-vouched is refused. Deduped events go through `logActivityOnce`.
│   ├── api/e/[token]/                     GET: the email click redirect (migration 0122). Looks the send up by `click_token` (service-role — the reader is usually signed out), writes ONE `email_clicked` (dedupe `email_click:<send id>`), 302s to `resolveClickTarget(?to)` — same-site paths only, else `/`. An unknown token still redirects and records nothing.
│   ├── api/admin/plans/                   POST (JSON, superadmin via requireSuperadmin). `list | upsert | setActive | saveSettings` → src/lib/billing/admin.ts (service-role). Every write revalidatePath()s /pricing, /terms, /refunds, /request-access.
│   ├── api/billing/order/                 POST (signed-in): reads the ACTIVE plan from `public.plans` and creates a Razorpay order whose notes are the CONTRACT (`stampOrderNotes`: userId, planId, amountPaise, currency, scope, durationDays); returns { orderId, amount, keyId, email }. 503 if keys unset.
│   ├── api/billing/verify/                POST (signed-in): verifies the Checkout success signature (HMAC order_id|payment_id), fetches the order from Razorpay and grants from its stamped NOTES (`planForPaidOrder` — never the plan's current price, so an edit cannot reject an open checkout). 202 if not yet captured. Idempotent with the webhook.
│   ├── api/billing/webhook/               POST (no auth — Razorpay calls it): reads RAW body, verifies x-razorpay-signature HMAC, then `decideWebhook`: `order.paid` grants from the stamped notes (same rule as verify), `refund.processed` revokes on a FULL refund. 200 on handled/ignored, 400 bad sig, 500 only on transient DB error (Razorpay retries).
│   ├── api/cart/preview/                  POST (JSON): { ids } → { items }. Slim per-question metadata (taxonomy + question_number + source_row + text) for the cart panel. RLS-scoped, capped at 200 ids.
│   ├── go/{learn,practice}/route.ts       cross-app remediation redirects (2026-06-17) — GET endpoints nda-tracker's exam/quiz reviews link to ("Learn this" / "Practice"). `learn` → the `/notes` subtopic page (concept anchor when given); `practice` → a `/browse` filter (corpus by subject: Maths→practice bank, else→PYQ). Resolve `subtopic` **slug-first** (quizzes + tagged exam q) then by **DB name** (exam path — names not slugs) via `lib/notes/goLinks`; every branch degrades to a sensible fallback. Dynamic; TDD `tests/go-routes.test.ts`. See [[cross-app-feature-execution]] + the 2026-06-17 Decisions log.
│   ├── api/export/                        POST → single file.
```
