```
├── reviews/                           **Post-ingestion review provenance** (migration 0074, 2026-08-11) — the record of who checked a question's answer and what they concluded, as distinct from `derived_model`/`derived_at` (0040), which records who *produced* a derived field and is upstream of anyone verifying it. `types.ts` — the closed `REVIEW_METHODS` / `REVIEW_VERDICTS` / `REVIEW_SOURCES` sets (mirrored by DB CHECKs) + labels + guards + `CORRECTIVE_VERDICTS` (the three meaning *we* were wrong, kept apart from `defect_preserved`, meaning the *source* was). `record.ts` — pure `sanitizeReviewRecord` / `sanitizeReviewRecords`; `reviewedContentHash` and `runLabel` are mandatory, an over-long note is rejected rather than truncated, and `source` defaults to `live` so a backfilled row must opt in. `staleness.ts` — `isReviewStale` (unknown current hash ⇒ stale, never fresh) + `latestReviewByQuestion` (append-only ⇒ newest row is current belief; first-wins on a timestamp tie, documented as a data problem). `summary.ts` — `summarizeRuns` → reviewed/confirmed/corrected/defects/unverifiable + `pctCorrected`, the four buckets partitioning the verdict set exactly. `triage.ts` — `resolveTriageReview` maps a report triage to a verdict ONLY where unambiguous (`wont-fix` on an answer-affecting category ⇒ `confirmed`) or explicitly picked; never inferred from a status alone. `service.ts` — `recordReview(s)` service-role upsert on the `(question_id, run_label, reviewed_content_hash)` constraint so a script re-run is a no-op; **never throws** (a lost audit row must not roll back the write it describes) but **never silent** either — `formatRecordResult` prints the hole. NOT marked `server-only` so the tsx scripts can import it.
│   ├── storage/
│   │   ├── images.ts                      uploadImage / deleteImage / downloadImage / validateImageUpload (server, uses node:crypto)
│   │   └── imageUrl.ts                    pure-function publicImageUrl — safe to import from client components
│   ├── text/{normalizeNewlines,safeSnippet}.ts   pure helpers. `normalizeNewlines` masks math zones (`\(…\)`, `\[…\]`, `$…$`, `$$…$$`) to opaque placeholders, replaces literal `\n` outside math with real newlines, restores math zones verbatim. Idempotent. Called from upload parser + edit validator so the DB never carries literal `\n` past the boundary; LaTeX commands like `\neq`, `\nu`, `\nabla` inside math are untouched. `safeSnippet(text, maxLen)` truncates with `…` but retreats if the cut would land inside a math delimiter — used by CartItem so cart preview snippets render via KatexRenderer without orphaning a half-open `\(`.
│   ├── export/pdf/                        **the PDF paper + key (2026-10-05)** for everyone without staff access. `paperHtml.ts` (pure, TDD `tests/pdf-paper-html.test.ts`): `buildPaperHtml`/`buildKeyHtml` → one A4 one-column HTML page through the SAME parsers as the site (`parseRichSegments`, `parseTableBlocks`, underline bypass), KaTeX on the server with mhchem, options four-across / two-by-two / stacked by printed length (`optionLayout`), a question never split across pages, watermark + `@page` margin-box footer (`www.pyqvault.com · Page N of M`; the printer's own footer template ignores fonts). `assets.ts` links the bundled fonts (`fonts/`: Source Serif 4 cut to fixed weights, Noto Sans Math, DejaVu Sans, Noto Serif Devanagari; OFL) and KaTeX's CSS as `file://`. `images.ts` fetches a document's pictures as data URIs (`imagePathsFor`: a paper never takes a solution picture). `printPdf.ts` drives Chromium over the DevTools protocol (no puppeteer): `@sparticuz/chromium` on Linux/Vercel, Edge on Windows, `PDF_BROWSER` to override; one print at a time per instance. The fonts, KaTeX files and Chromium binary reach the function only via `outputFileTracingIncludes` in next.config.mjs. Sample: `npm run pdf:sample`.
│   └── export/{ommlBuilder,docxBuilder,imageDimensions,groupBySet,stripPassageCount,tagsSheet,sourceTag}.ts   LaTeX → OMML, .docx assembly. **`sourceTag.ts`** (2026-07-28, pure, TDD `tests/export-source-tag.test.ts`): `formatSourceTag({exam,pyqYear})` → `[JEE Mains 2016]`, the coaching-book citation `buildQuestionPaper` prints after a stem when `includeSourceTag` is set (off by default → existing exports byte-identical). Exam **name + year only** — `pyq_note` is a clean label only for NDA (`"NDA 1"`/`"NDA 2"`), inconsistent within JEE (`"Paper 7"` AND `"29 June 2022"`) and a provenance sentence for MH-SSC-10. Returns null when `pyqYear` is null, which bank-wide is exactly the `question_kind='practice'` rows — so a mixed paper tags its PYQs and never credits a practice question to a sitting. Distinct from `lib/questions/formatProvenance.ts` (the web card's `Q# · month · year` chip). The tag rides on the LAST prose block of the stem, before options; a stem ending in a GFM pipe-table gets it as its own paragraph. Answer key never carries it. Contract pinned by `tests/docx-source-tag.test.ts`. **`tagsSheet.ts`** (2026-06-14, pure, TDD `tests/tags-sheet.test.ts`): `buildTagRows(QuestionRow[])` → TagRow[] for nda-tracker's `parseTagsFile` — numbers via the SAME `groupBySet` loop the docx uses (Q-numbers match the printed paper + Evalbee scan by construction), derives `Answer` from the `isCorrect` option, maps subjects to nda-tracker keys (`mapSubjectToTracker`: Mathematics→Maths, Current Affairs→Others), and emits a distinct `Context` column for passage sets (never inlined). **`SubtopicSlug` + `ConceptSlug` columns (2026-06-17)** carry each question's primary `question_concept_tags` row (the export route builds the map via `getResourceTagsForQuestions`; empty for untagged English/GK/practice) so nda-tracker can build slug-precise remediation links — `buildTagRows(questions, conceptTags?)` stays pure (optional second arg). **`QuestionId` column (2026-09-11)** carries the row's `questions.id` so an nda-tracker exam result can be traced back to the bank item (measured difficulty, exposure control, key corrections). **Gated to a real uuid** by `bankIdOrBlank` — `recToQuestionRow` (`scripts/practice-paper/config.ts`) feeds this same builder rows synthesized from a JSON spec with ids like `eng-geo-19aug-7`, and emitting one would be provenance that resolves to nothing; the concept-tag join still uses the raw id. A SOFT reference (separate Supabase projects, no FK) — nda-tracker must degrade to name-based behaviour when it is blank. **The DERIVATIONS now live in `src/lib/sync/questionPayload.ts`** (2026-09-11) — `mapSubjectToTracker` (re-exported here, its original home), `difficultyLabel`, `answerLabel`, `optionText`, `subtopicName` — shared with the JSON transports (`GET /api/questions/by-ids`, and the paper push to come). THE shape is `questionPayload.ts`: field names are **nda-tracker's** so hydration there is a merge not a translation, absent is `null`, and images resolve to **absolute** public urls because the DB stores a storage path our UI wraps at render. The two containers legitimately differ (the sheet has no image columns and writes `""` where JSON writes `null`); what a subject maps to and which option is the answer must not. The read route is **authenticated and org-scoped, never anonymous** — anon RLS sees only PUBLIC rows and much of the bank is PRIVATE, so an anonymous endpoint would return nothing for exactly the papers that matter, silently, and could not serve a second institute; the Bearer secret resolves through `tracker_sync_targets` (migration 0094) to an org. Contract doc: nda-tracker `CROSS_APP_SYNC.md`. `tagRowsToAoa` → array-of-arrays for SheetJS (`TAG_COLUMNS` header). The `/api/export` `kind:"tags"` handler is the only consumer of the route; `scripts/practice-paper/build-tags*.ts` share the builder. **Layout (2026-05-25 onwards):** numbering is **flush-left** (`{left: 0, hanging: 0}` + `LevelSuffix.SPACE`) so the question number, wrap lines, options, and Context all sit at column 0 — no hanging indent. Option labels render **lowercase** `(a)/(b)/(c)/(d)` in both question paper and answer key; bank still stores uppercase `A/B/C/D`, the lowercasing happens at render only. A **blank paragraph** sits between every question (singles + set siblings) so the student's eye has a visible boundary; the solution answer-key adds the same blank between each (answer + solution) block (plain answer key stays compact). ommlBuilder.sanitizeOmmlForXml escape-pass over `<m:t>` text content (mml2omml emits raw `<` for LaTeX comparators like `0 < α < 90`, which Word rejects), then `wrapMatrixDelimiters` (2026-06-03) rewrites a delimited matrix's bare flanking fence runs (`pmatrix` `(`, `bmatrix` `[`, `vmatrix` `|` determinant, `Bmatrix` `{`, `Vmatrix` `‖`) into a stretchy `<m:d>` delimiter object — mml2omml emits the fence operators as plain single-line `<m:r><m:t>(</m:t></m:r>` runs that Word draws tiny and detached beside the grid; only a fence run immediately adjacent to `<m:m>` is consumed so ordinary `f(x)` math and fence-less `\begin{matrix}` are untouched. docxBuilder iterates groupBySet with **student-first set-banner rendering** (2026-05-25): set-of-1 in the export drops the `Set:` framing entirely and renders the passage as inline `Context:` for the lone sibling; set-of-2+ emits `Common context for questions X-Y:` where X-Y is the 1-indexed position of the first + last sibling in the export (computed from a running position counter, so the banner names the actual question numbers wherever the set lands), and the passage is run through `stripPassageCountPhrase` to remove the verbatim "for the three (03) items that follow" claim so the banner doesn't contradict the actual count when the user has selected a subset. Bank text stays verbatim in DB; only the render is normalised. After `Packer.toBuffer`, `patchZip` post-processes both `word/document.xml` (marker-run → OMML) and `word/settings.xml` (injects `<m:mathPr>` with `defJc=left` + `wrapIndent=0` + zero margins so fraction-containing options don't get shifted right by Word's built-in math defaults). imageDimensions reads natural width/height from PNG IHDR or JPEG SOF and scale-to-fits within column-width caps (320×360 for question diagrams, 200×200 for option images), preserving aspect ratio. Falls back to the legacy fixed rectangle if a header can't be parsed. **`stripPassageCount.ts`** — pure helper, regex strip of `for (the next |the )?(two|three|…|\d+)(\s*\(\d+\))?\s*items? that follows?` → `for the items that follow` (or `…that follows` for singular). 10 unit tests cover the common phrasings + idempotency + LaTeX-zone preservation.
├── components/
│   ├── AppHeader.tsx + header/HeaderBar.tsx + UserMenu.tsx + ThemeToggle.tsx + PrimaryNav.tsx + GoogleSignInButton.tsx   sticky 56px header (Tier 1 IA, 2026-05-18). **Restructured 2026-07-29 into a cacheable server shell + a client bar — this component was why NOTHING on the site had ever been cached.** It used to resolve the session (three separate `auth.getUser()` calls) and read the `qb_exam` cookie during server render; both are per-request, so Next marked EVERY route dynamic and the build produced **zero** prerendered HTML files, silently voiding the `revalidate` on 77 notes chapters, 10 guides, the homepage and the mock catalogue. Now `AppHeader` is a thin server shell whose only server work is the cached, visitor-independent slug→exam-uuid map (`lib/exam/examIdMap.ts`), and **`header/HeaderBar.tsx` (client)** owns everything per-visitor: the session (fetched once from the no-store `GET /api/me/header`, skipped entirely when no Supabase cookie is present), the `qb_exam` cookie read, the brand-link destination, the Papers-tab gate and the nav hrefs (pure `lib/exam/examNav.ts`). Result: 622 prerendered pages, and the server HTML contains no user data — which is a correctness requirement, not just perf, since a cached page is one copy served to everyone. **`ExamPill.tsx` was DELETED 2026-08-21** — the header no longer offers an exam switcher. `qb_exam` survives, written only by /welcome + /account (both behind sign-in), so a MISSING cookie now means "anonymous, never chose" and `resolveExamNav` returns the neutral indexes rather than defaulting to NDA — which would otherwise strand an anon JEE visitor in the NDA bank with no control left to fix it. Bank/Guides/Board still personalise from the cookie; **Notes never does** (`/notes/<slug>` is a "coming soon" dead end for 10 of 13 exams). See the 2026-08-21 Decisions entry. Renders: brand link, `PrimaryNav`, `ThemeToggle`, and (as of 2026-06-01) `UserMenu` for **any signed-in user** — org chip only for members; role `null` = "Student" — else a Sign-in button; a fixed-size placeholder holds the account slot while identity loads. See [[shell-component-decaches-site]] + [[next-caching-traps]]. UserMenu items: **Saved questions** (→ /saved, 2026-07-12) + **Account & access** (→ /account) + Sign out (→ /browse). `GoogleSignInButton` (client) drives Supabase Google OAuth through /api/auth/callback; used on /login + /signup. `ThemeToggle` reads/writes `localStorage["theme"]` and toggles `.dark` on `<html>`.
│   ├── exam/ExamFeedList.tsx + TierExamChips.tsx   the exam-tier UI (2026-09-25). `ExamFeedList` is the client island on `/mock`, `/notes`, `/guide`, `/board`: the server passes ALREADY-RENDERED cards + their slugs, the island moves "Your exams" first and collapses the rest under "Other exams (N)". `TierExamChips` = the stage-filtered target-exam chips on `/welcome` + `/account` (`examChipsForTier`), rest behind "Show all exams". `/me` adds `me/StageNudge.tsx` (1 Apr – 30 Jun, `needsStageNudge`).
│   ├── auth/useSignedIn.ts                shared client hook (2026-07-12) — reads the Supabase session in-browser (`getSession`, local, no round-trip) + tracks auth changes → `{signedIn, loading}`. Used by the notes practice gate, reveal meter, and progress controls INSTEAD of a server read so pages stay ISR-static. Promoted here from notes/_components (now app-wide).
│   ├── reveal/{useRevealMeter,RevealSignInPrompt}.tsx   client answer-reveal meter (2026-07-12): `useRevealMeter` wraps `useSignedIn` + a localStorage set (`qb_revealed`) over the pure `revealDecision` → `attemptReveal(id)`; `RevealSignInPrompt` is the wall shown once the free budget's spent (browse + board). See [[project-saved-and-reveal-meter]].
│   ├── reveal/{SignInLink.tsx,signInHref.ts}   sign-in link that returns to this page (2026-10-04): the `useSearchParams` read sits behind its OWN Suspense with a path-only fallback, so a question list still prerenders (tests/sign-in-link.test.ts allow-lists every `useSearchParams` import)
│   ├── billing/useCheckout.ts             the one Razorpay checkout for /pricing + the download box (2026-10-04): order (with its gate) → Razorpay → verify; emits checkout_opened/paid/failed/dismissed with a surface
│   ├── OfflineBanner.tsx                  client island listening to `online`/`offline` events; renders a sticky-bottom destructive strip with the WifiOff icon when offline. Mounted globally in layout.tsx so every route gets it. Respects `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` for iOS.
│   ├── nav/Breadcrumbs.tsx                THE site breadcrumb (2026-10-07): a 36 px round Home button, then blue links, current page bold. GuideShell (guides, notes, mocks, blog) and every other page with a trail render it; `tests/breadcrumb-single-source.test.ts` fails if any other file draws a breadcrumb `<nav>`, because seven hand-rolled copies had drifted into six styles.
│   ├── Footer.tsx                         site-wide footer: tagline + FOUR link columns (Explore · Guides · Notes · About), rendered from the pure `lib/nav/footerLinks.ts` model. **Reshaped 2026-09-24** — it had grown to 23 hand-typed links in one flat wrap (11 individual subject guides, an icon each, a GitHub link) and listed 2 of the 4 /notes hubs. Guides and Notes columns now DERIVE one link per exam hub from `getGuideExamGroups`/`getNotesExamGroups`, so a new exam's hub appears by itself; subject guides are one hop away on the hub. No GitHub link (user's call). "How it works" → `/start` so an anon phone reader with no account menu can find it. Sits in the cached shell — no cookies. Pinned by `tests/footer-links.test.ts` (shape) + `tests/crawl-entry-points.test.ts` (the crawl-budget surfaces, read from the model)
│   ├── math/{parseLatex,parseTableBlocks,KatexRenderer,RichText,BlockText}.tsx   shared LaTeX/text renderers. `parseLatex` (math segmenter — also used by the Word-export `ommlBuilder`, so kept unchanged) plus pure exports: `splitBold` (`**bold**` → runs) + **`splitItalic`** (`*italic*` inside non-bold runs, STRICT flanking rule, a span must hold a letter/digit; 2026-10-07, read by all five outputs via the `italic` run flag, pinned by `tests/render-italic-contract.test.ts`) + `parseRichText` (blocks: paragraphs + `- ` bullet lists, math-masked; 2026-05-29) + **`maskMathZones`** (mask `\(...\)`/`$...$` to PUA sentinels + an `unmask`; 2026-06-02, used by the table parser). **`parseTableBlocks`** (2026-06-02, pure, math-masked) splits text into prose + GFM-table blocks (header + `|---|` separator row = the table/math discriminator). `KatexRenderer` renders inline `**bold**` everywhere it's used (browse/guide/notes); `RichText` is the block renderer (paragraphs + bullet lists + bold + math) used for notes `definition` fields; **`BlockText`** is the table-aware renderer (prose via KatexRenderer + real `<table>`) used for question `text`/`context` on /browse + editor preview + the /notes mastery-check / /guide worked-example `WorkedExampleCard` (2026-06-04) (see the "Tables in question content" convention). **The /browse SET BANNER's shared passage, the worked-example SOLUTION and the editor's solution preview were still on `KatexRenderer` until 2026-09-24** (61 sets printed their match-the-columns table as raw pipes); `tests/long-form-field-renderer-contract.test.ts` now scans every .tsx for a `KatexRenderer` fed a `context`/`passage`/`solution` expression and fails on any — the only render assertion this jsdom-less repo can make.
│   └── ui/{button,input,label,card,badge,select,dialog,sheet,skeleton,stepper,dropzone}.tsx   shadcn primitives
└── middleware.ts                          Supabase session refresh + /dashboard guard

supabase/
├── migrations/0001..0047_*.sql            apply in order via Supabase MCP. Per-migration one-liners below (0001–0047; CLAUDE.md's `supabase/migrations/` line carries the same list with more prose on the recent ones).
│       · 0009 — visibility enum + public-read policies (default PRIVATE at the time; flipped to PUBLIC in 0022)
│       · 0010 — sync metadata columns (pyq_year, marks, neg_marks, attempt_stats, source_mock_id, source_app)
│       · 0011 — rate_limits table + atomic increment function
│       · 0012 — partial index on (visibility, exam_id, subject_id, created_at desc) WHERE visibility='PUBLIC' (public hot path)
│       · 0013 — questions.upload_job_id FK ON DELETE SET NULL + partial index
│       · 0014 — data backfill of upload_job_id by (org_id, source_file) when ownership is unambiguous
│       · 0015 — pyq_month + pyq_note text columns on questions
│       · 0016 — question_number text column (original Q-number from Excel "Q" cell)
│       · 0017 — set_id text column + partial index for grouping passage-set siblings (format `<upload_job_id>:<excel-set-label>`, scoped per upload)
│       · 0018 — `get_dashboard_stats(p_org_id)` RPC returning `{total_questions, chapters_covered, by_exam}` as jsonb — bypasses PostgREST's implicit 1000-row cap
│       · 0019 — `get_pyq_years()` SQL aggregate function for the PYQ-year filter chip list
│       · 0020 — `get_chapter_facets()` + `get_subtopic_facets()` RPCs powering "Chapter (N)" / "Subtopic (N)" rendering in the /browse sidebar (context-aware: counts reflect all OTHER active filters)
│       · 0021 — `question_concept_tags` table (composite PK on (question_id, subtopic_slug, concept_slug)) linking bank questions to /notes concept slugs; RLS inherits question visibility (anon reads tags only on PUBLIC questions; ADMIN writes scoped to own-org questions). **Cost note:** because subtopic_slug is part of the PK, renaming a subtopic_slug (Phase D restructure of an already-tagged chapter) invalidates every row — they must be DELETEd and reinserted with the new slugs. Cheap when Phase D runs BEFORE the first tag (the Probability and Sound-redesign pattern); paid twice when Phase D runs AFTER tagging (the Sound bank-first regression). See 2026-05-30 (redesign) decisions log.
│       · 0022 — `questions.visibility` default flipped PRIVATE → PUBLIC + UPDATE flipped remaining 120 PRIVATE rows
│       · 0023 — `question_principle_tags` table (composite PK on (question_id, principle_slug)) linking bank questions to /guide TOP_11 principle slugs; parallel RLS to 0021 — drives live DB-backed counts on `/guide/nda-maths/principles` and the `?principle=<slug>` /browse filter
│       · 0024 — `question_reports` table + `report_category` (8 values) + `report_status` (5 values) enums for in-app reporting. Denormalized `org_id` from question.org_id at INSERT (helper-set, not trigger) so admin triage queries use one indexed lookup. Partial unique index on `(reported_by, question_id) WHERE status='open'` blocks duplicate open reports. 4 RLS policies: insert authenticated-as-self · select-own-reports · select-admin-own-org · update-admin-own-org. No DELETE policy — reports kept for audit.
│       · 0025 — Teacher editor role. New helper `private.current_user_can_edit_questions()` (true for `org_members.role IN ('ADMIN','TEACHER')`). Rewrites `questions UPDATE` → editor; splits the old `admin write options` FOR ALL into `admin insert options` + `editor update options` + `admin delete options`. Storage policies on `question-images` bucket opened from admin-only → editor (TEACHER + ADMIN can upload/update/delete in own-org folder). INSERT + DELETE on `questions` stay admin-only; `upload_jobs` stays admin-only; `chapters`/`subtopics` insert stays admin-only (no taxonomy auto-create by teachers). Adds `last_edited_by uuid references auth.users(id)` + `last_edited_at timestamptz` columns on `questions` + a partial index `(last_edited_at desc) where last_edited_at is not null`. Populated by `applyEdit` when the route hands it a user id.
│       · 0026 — `entitlements` table (per-USER premium access, keyed on auth.users(id), INDEPENDENT of orgs). Cols: scope ('all' = full premium), source ('razorpay'|'comp'|'manual'), status, expires_at, provider_ref, note, granted_by. RLS: `entitlements_select_own` (user reads own); **no write policy → service-role-only writes** (Razorpay webhook + comp-admin UI). Partial index on user_id WHERE status='active'.
│       · 0027 — partial UNIQUE index on `entitlements(provider_ref) WHERE source='razorpay'` — idempotency so a single Razorpay payment grants at most once (client-verify + webhook can't double-grant; the loser hits 23505 → "already_granted").
│       · 0028 — `concept_reports` table (per-CONCEPT reports on /notes; migration 0028 / 2026-06-02). New `concept_report_category` enum; **REUSES `report_status`** from 0024. Concept identity is the slug pair `(subtopic_slug, concept_slug)` (no FK — concepts are TS editorial, not rows) + denormalized breadcrumb/route segments. `org_id` (NOT NULL → organizations) is helper-resolved from a PUBLIC question in the concept's subtopic since the taxonomy is org-less. Partial unique index on `(reported_by, subtopic_slug, concept_slug) WHERE status='open'`. 4 RLS policies identical in shape to `question_reports` (insert-self · reporter-read-own · admin-read-own-org · admin-update-own-org). No DELETE policy.
│       · 0029 — `subtopics.order_index int` (NULLABLE, default NULL; migration 0029 / 2026-06-06). Pedagogical teaching order within a chapter, populated for /notes chapters by `scripts/sync-subtopic-order.ts` (`npm run notes:order`). NULL = unset → sorts last in both the JS facet sort + the SQL question sort, so non-noted chapters keep count-desc.
│       · 0030 — `quiz_atoms` — the Daily Quiz Factory MCQ-atom pool (harvested from /notes ConceptUnits; status needs_review→verified).
│       · 0031 — `quizzes` + `quiz_atoms_map` (an assembled quiz ↔ its atoms).
│       · 0032 — `quiz_atoms.theme` (group atoms by theme for assembly).
│       · 0033 — `quizzes.public_slug` + `quiz_leads` (the public lead-magnet quiz funnel; mobile = the lead, never an account).
│       · 0034 — `record_quiz_lead` retake-aware upsert RPC (roll a lead up by mobile).
│       · 0035 — `quizzes.questions` jsonb + `theme` (immutable quiz snapshot — display decoupled from the live atom pool).
│       · 0036 — `questions.question_kind` enum (`pyq`|`practice`, default pyq) — the axis separating the PYQ corpus from the supplementary practice bank.
│       · 0037 — `p_kind` param on the facet RPCs (the /browse PYQ/Practice/All toggle).
│       · 0038 — **per-exam dedup**: swap the `questions` unique key `(org_id, content_hash)` → `(org_id, exam_id, content_hash)` so CDS↔NDA shared UPSC questions keep both copies.
│       · 0039 — `papers` + `paper_questions` junction (collaborative paper builder, org-scoped RLS, finalize-snapshot).
│       · 0040 — **RAG grounding layer**: `pgvector` (extensions schema) + `questions.plain_text`/`solution_json`/`derived_model`/`derived_at` + a generalized `embeddings` table (unsized `vector`, no index) + `match_chunks` RPC (JOIN-guarded PUBLIC+pyq). See [[rag-grounding-layer]].
│       · 0041 — `question_format` enum (`mcq`|`subjective`, default mcq) — subjective/free-response questions (State Board textbook content: zero options, answer in `solution`).
│       · 0042 — `questions.solution_image_url` (nullable) — an optional per-question SOLUTION diagram, shown in the /browse model-answer reveal + docx answer-key only (never the question paper).
│       · 0086 — **`books` + `book_questions`** — curated, orderable PYQ books over questions already in the bank. MATERIALISED membership (one row per question, fractional `position`) rather than sparse overrides, which collapse the moment anything is reordered. `excluded` is a FLAG, never a deleted row: if exclusion meant absence, absence would mean both "never seen" and "deliberately dropped", and the next sync would silently re-add everything rejected. `chapter_slug` is the BOOK's structure, not the bank's — they start equal and phase 3 lets them diverge. Locked RLS (enabled, NO policies → service-role only), the `platform_admins`/`entitlements` pattern; expect the `rls_enabled_no_policy` advisor INFO. See [[project-books-tab]].
│       · 0043 — **book-structure section columns** for `/board`: `section_kind` enum (`solved_example`|`exercise`|`miscellaneous`) + `section_group`/`section_label`/`section_seq` (nullable; only board/textbook rows carry them). ORTHOGONAL to the conceptual `subtopic` axis. See [[board-reader]].
│       · 0044 — **timed mock tests**: `mock_tests` (immutable ordered question-ref snapshot + exam-pattern config; service-role writes, published readable by all) + `mock_attempts` (per-user own-row RLS, retakes, partial-unique one-in-progress-per-mock) + `attempt_answers` (parent-attempt EXISTS RLS). See [[mock-tests]].
│       · 0088 — **mock `source` + `scope`** (2026-09-07): two independent axes on `mock_tests` (`source` pyq|practice, `scope` full|sectional), both DEFAULTing to what all 187 existing rows are, so the migration is a no-op and the build script keeps working untouched. `pyq_year` becomes NULLABLE (an assembled paper has no sitting, and that field is rendered as a section HEADING — a stand-in year would print a sitting that never happened), guarded by `mock_tests_full_pyq_has_year`: only a paper claiming to be a whole real sitting must name it, since a SECTIONAL test built from PYQs draws on many. Proven to fire in both directions before use. No new index — 187 rows, and this project has already traced disk-IO exhaustion to indexes added on reasoning rather than measurement.
│       · 0045 — `student_profiles` — per-student post-signup contact mobile keyed on `auth.users(id)`, own-row RLS (student writes via JWT). Powers the mock-result phone-capture gate. See [[project-student-mobile-capture]].
│       · 0046 — `notes_progress` (per-student /notes track: bookmark · mastered_at · latest checkpoint self-score · last_viewed_at; 2026-07-12). Own-row RLS on all verbs (student writes via JWT). Keyed on editorial `subtopic_slug` + denormalized `chapter_slug`/`subject_route` (notes are TS, no FK — soft ref like `concept_reports`). CHECK on checkpoint coherence. See [[project-notes-signin]].
│       · 0047 — `question_bookmarks` (saved questions; 2026-07-12). Own-row RLS (select/insert/delete, no UPDATE — existence IS the bookmark). **Real FK** `question_id → questions(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE (unlike notes' soft slug refs). Index `(user_id, created_at DESC)`. See [[project-saved-and-reveal-meter]].
│       · All RPCs are `security invoker` so RLS still applies (anon → PUBLIC counts only; authenticated → PUBLIC + own-org PRIVATE).
└── seed/
    ├── taxonomy.json                      committed snapshot from MHT_CET_2025_PCM.xlsx
    └── seed-first-org.sql                 manual onboarding for first admin

scripts/
├── jee/                                   JEE Mains DOCX→bank ingestion pipeline (per-paper, parameterized by `<paperId>`). pandoc OMML→LaTeX + media → existing `commitStaged`. `lib.ts` (pure, TDD'd: segment/parse/sanitize/de-glue) · `config.ts` (IDs + `PaperData` + `loadPaper` + `isCommittable`) · `papers/<paperId>.json` (curated per-paper classification + stem/answer/option overrides + authored solutions) · `extract`/`commit`/`attach-images`/`attach-solutions`/`cleanup-latex` (upright-funcs + KaTeX-validated de-glue + split-`\(…\)`-delimiter rebalance + trailing-`\` strip, recomputing hashes)/`validate-db` (KaTeX + markup-leak + dangling-delimiter + trailing-`\` + soft completeness scans) · **`resync`** (re-apply overrides to committed rows + recompute hash WITHOUT re-committing — a re-commit after cleanup drifts hashes → duplicates). `out/` artifacts gitignored. Loop in `scripts/jee/README.md`. See [[jee-mains-ingestion]] + [[docx-pandoc-ingestion]].
├── reviews/                              `paper.ts` (`npm run reviews:paper`) — dump a paper's questions for pre-print solution review and record the verdicts back (`--record --apply`), run label `paper:<id>`. Skips questions already confirmed by evidence at least as strong as the pass being run (`--method=solution_audit` default, or `blind_rederivation`), and **withholds the key + stored solution when the method is blind** so "independent" means it. Surfaces each question's shared `context` (a set-based question reads as unanswerable without it) and REFUSES to overwrite a dump holding filled verdicts without `--force`. `resolve-report.ts` (`npm run reviews:resolve`) — resolve a student question-report from the CLI, recording the triage verdict via the same `recordTriageReview` the admin route calls (most reports are resolved from a script, so a web-only recorder missed the common path). `backfill.ts` (`npm run reviews:backfill`, **dry-run by default**) — reconstructs review provenance from the 55 committed `<id>.crosscheck.json` / `<id>.mcq-verify.json` artifacts (844 rows → 834 stored, all `source='backfilled'`). Ref-only rows resolve via `(source_file, question_number)`; id-bearing rows compare their blind derivation against the LIVE key. **Every ambiguity is a BLOCKING error, never a guess**: an unmappable verdict (`OUR-ANSWER-WRONG`), a derivation disagreeing with the live key, a verdict CONFLICT within one run (two overlapping agent batches reaching different verdicts — the dedupe key would otherwise let file order decide), or a stale entry in `backfill-overrides.json` that matches nothing. `backfill-overrides.json` holds the 13 adjudications, each citing its Decisions-log date; its `_`-prefixed keys are documentation blocks. Idempotent via the 0074 dedupe constraint. `report.ts` (`npm run reviews:report`) — read-only review-coverage report over `question_reviews` (0074). Three sections: **coverage** (PUBLIC questions per exam/subject with at least one review row), **per-run rollup** (reviewed / confirmed / corrected / defects / unverifiable / %corrected — `corrected` vs `defects` is the "the textbook key is wrong ~4× as often as our answers" claim as data), and **stale reviews** (the stored fingerprint ≠ the question's current `content_hash`). Totals come from the `get_chapter_facets` aggregate called on the **anon** client — RLS scopes it to PUBLIC, which is the corpus the coverage claim is about — never a row-derived `.select()`; review rows are paged. Copy is deliberate: a 0 reads "NOT RECORDED", which for anything predating the table is not the same as unexamined. `--exam=<name>` scopes it, `--stale` prints only that section.
├── imat/notes/                           IMAT notes tooling (2026-10-09). `AUTHORING_BRIEF.md` (the contract every chapter is written to) · `dump-chapters.ts` (one evidence file per chapter from `data/<year>.questions.json` → gitignored `generated-papers/imat-notes/`) · `bank.ts` (chapter → subject + counts, the taxonomy the test checks against) · `check-chapter.ts <subject>/<folder>` (the contract on an UNREGISTERED folder) · `show-selfchecks.ts` (every self-check + worked answer, for a reviewer to re-derive) · `register.py` (adds a folder to the registry) · `render-smoke.tsx` (server-renders every concept card and hidden step; run with `--tsconfig scripts/imat/notes/tsconfig.render.json --require ./scripts/imat/notes/ignore-css.cjs`). `notes-latex-audit.ts` and `notes-arc.ts` take `--imat`.
├── relevance/                            **syllabus-fit screen** commit pipeline (migration 0062, 2026-07-27). `commit.ts` — reads `data/<screen>.json` (`{audience, scope, screenedOn, exclusions:[{questionId, blockingTool, note}]}`), **dry-run by default**, validates every `blockingTool` against `BLOCKING_TOOLS` in `src/lib/relevance/config.ts` (the DB column is free text on purpose — a CHECK'd enum would mean a migration per chapter screened — so this is the only gate) and every `questionId` against the live bank, then idempotently upserts on `(question_id, audience)`. Screening a chapter takes **BOTH** steps: commit its exclusions here AND append the chapter to `REVIEWED_CHAPTERS`; committing only the first leaves the chapter looking unscreened, appending only the second silently marks its drops as passing (the script prints the reminder). `data/jee-maths-matrices-determinants.json` = the first screen (251 q → 12 exclusions). See [[syllabus-fit-screen]].
├── formula/                              the formula-axis pipeline, **chapter by chapter**. `harvest.ts` (`npm run formula:harvest -- "Chapter" [...]`) pools a chapter's MCQs across exams (the same material is `Matrices & Determinants` in NDA, `Determinants and Matrices` in MHT-CET, split in CBSE) into 80-question batches in a stable order, so a half-finished pass resumes. **Then every solution is READ** and tagged with the identities it invokes → `tags/<chapter>.json`, the expensive artifact, tracked precisely because no machine produced it. `meta/<chapter>.ts` holds the authored wording; a slug with no META entry is simply not published, which is how the long tail and `direct-computation` are withheld. `generate-registry.ts` (`npm run formula:generate`) is PURE — tags + meta → `src/lib/formula/<chapter>.ts`, no DB, deterministic, refusing if META names a slug the tags lack. **12-question publishing floor.** `score-classifier.ts` (`npm run formula:score`) re-runs the proof that this cannot be automated: against all 768 pilot labels a signature classifier gets **59.2% precision / 45.0% recall**, 1 of 79 identities clearing 90/85 — naming an operator is not using an identity, and an identity is routinely used unnamed. Runbook `scripts/formula/README.md`.
├── practice/                              NDA practice-question (NOT PYQ) ingestion — born-digital PDFs with a LOSSY text layer, so VISION-driven (per-column render → transcribe → commitStaged). `config.ts` (IDs + `Topic` type + `TOPICS` registry + paths) · `lib.ts` (pure, TDD'd in `tests/practice-extract.test.ts`: `parseAnswerKey`/`buildRecords`/`findLatexImbalance`) · `render.ts` (pages → per-column high-DPI PNGs in gitignored `out/`) · `commit.ts` (merge `data/<topic>.{questions,solutions,overrides}.json` + parsed answer key → `commitStaged` PRIVATE → post-commit UPDATE stamps `question_kind='practice'`) · `preview.ts` (KaTeX review HTML). `data/*.json` = committed transcription (source of truth). Runbook `scripts/practice/README.md`. See [[practice-ingestion]] + [[practice-pdf-vision-ingestion]].
├── cds/                                   CDS English PYQ ingestion — scanned image booklets (no text layer, **NO answer key**), so VISION-driven + answers **LLM-DERIVED + confidence-flagged**, committed PRIVATE for spot-check. Section order/selection varies by year → a per-paper **SECTION-MAP** pre-pass, then transcribe **BY SECTION** (the Directions drive the task type + can flip the answer). `config.ts` (PAPERS registry + the section-type catalog `SECTION_CATALOG` — each CDS section type → NDA-English taxonomy + `underline`/`inlineStimulus`/`passage`/`perQuestionSubtopic` flags) · `lib.ts` (pure, TDD'd in `tests/cds-lib.test.ts`: `buildRecords` directions→context + section-as-set + underline markup + fold-stimulus-into-stem · `validateRows` coverage/collision/delimiter · `normalizeQuestions` self-heal object-form options + difficulty casing) · `render.ts` (pages → PNGs in gitignored `out/`) · `commit.ts` (build → `commitStaged` PRIVATE → flip visibility) · `preview.ts` (review HTML). `data/<paperId>.{sections,questions,underlines}.json` = committed transcription (source of truth). Runbook `scripts/cds/README.md`. **PUBLIC + 19 timed mocks 2026-08-25**, adding `flip-public.ts` (exam_id-scoped; structural gate fails closed; warns on duplicate options, separating key-in-group from distractor-only; asserts a bank-wide PUBLIC delta) · `apply-key-fixes.ts` (20 adjudicated key corrections as data, each asserting BOTH the current and the expected key; idempotent) · `resync.ts` (deletes rows whose `content_hash` no longer matches the JSON — a repair mints a new hash so a re-commit INSERTS the fix and leaves the original; computes expected hashes through the REAL commit pipeline, and **refuses if a stale row is used in a teacher paper**) · `verify-keys.ts` · `verify-mocks.ts` (asserts every mock ref resolves to a live PUBLIC gradeable row — a PRIVATE ref renders BLANK with no error and grades every answer wrong) · `matchlist-verify.ts` · `audit-keys.ts` (CDS-scoped; `npm run audit:keys` is hard-filtered to `question_kind='practice'`). `commit.ts` now REFUSES to silently un-publish a live paper (its force-PRIVATE step is paper-wide, not insert-only) without `--allow-unpublish`. See [[cds-english-ingestion]] + [[blind-rederivation-needs-option-fidelity]] + [[section-based-exam-ingestion]].
├── foundation/                            Foundation Course (LWS NDA-Foundation Class 9/10 NCERT Science worksheets) ingestion — a **practice-only** corpus (`question_kind='practice'`), VISION-driven; **no answer keys** → every answer DERIVED + lower-confidence ones `REVIEW`-flagged in `overrides.json`; figure-bearing. `config.ts` (FOUNDATION exam id + `WORKSHEETS` registry; reuses `scripts/practice/lib.ts` pure helpers) · `render.ts` (per-column hi-DPI PNGs) · `commit.ts` (merge `data/<id>.{questions,overrides}.json` → `commitStaged` PRIVATE → post-commit UPDATE stamps `question_kind='practice'`) · `attach-images.ts` (manifest-driven figure crop from `data/<id>.figures.json` → `uploadImage` → `image_url`; a figure split across non-contiguous regions → a Pillow stitched composite attached via a one-off) · `docx-to-pdf.ts` (**Word-COM** docx→PDF for docx-only worksheets) · `preview.ts` (KaTeX review HTML). `data/*.json` = committed transcription (source of truth). REVIEW-flag re-verification is a separate batched audit (parallel sub-agents re-derive + human adjudicates flips / flawed→PRIVATE — direct DB update, NOT re-commit, which would orphan figure `image_url`s). See [[foundation-course]] + [[practice-pdf-vision-ingestion]].
├── practice-paper/                        LWS **test-paper ingestion** (the `/lws-test-ingest` skill) — a teacher-authored printed test PDF → THREE deliverables: the nda-tracker tagged-enrichment XLSX (ALL q in printed/OMR order), deduped PRIVATE `question_kind='practice'` bank rows, and a `/dashboard/papers` paper (the full printed test). `config.ts` (PAPERS registry + `data/<slug>.records.json` transcription + `recToQuestionRow`/`recToParsedRow` adapters + `validateRecords`) · `build-tags.ts <slug>` (the tagged XLSX → `generated-papers/`) · `commit-paper.ts <slug> --apply` (PRIVATE practice rows + the paper via service-role `createPaper`/`addQuestion`) · `flip-public.ts <slug> --apply` (promote only `status:"new"` rows → PUBLIC; `dup`/`flawed` stay PRIVATE forever). Plus a legacy one-off `build.ts` (assembles a fixed 120-q NDA practice paper into 2 .docx via the website export pipeline) + `types.ts`. See [[lws-test-paper-ingest]].
├── stateboard/                            Maharashtra State Board textbook (Balbharati Class 11/12) ingestion — a **practice-only** corpus mixing **MCQ and SUBJECTIVE** questions (migration 0041 `question_format`). A chapter = 3 buckets: solved examples (WITH the book's solution → ship PUBLIC), exercise MCQs (answer DERIVED), exercise-subjective (answer pending). HYBRID extraction: text for stems/options/prose, **VISION for truth-table solutions → GFM pipe-tables** + figures. Set-grouping: a `Q.N` instruction + `i)/ii)/iii…` sub-items → shared `context` + `setLabel` siblings. `config.ts` (exam id + chapter registry) · `lib.ts` (pure dual-format + set-grouping record core + `subjectiveContentHash`; TDD `tests/stateboard-lib.test.ts`) · `render.ts` (full-page PNGs — **mostly TWO-column and it varies per page within a chapter**; agents read left column fully then right, and verify per page themselves rather than trusting a derived column map) · per-section vision-transcribed `data/<id>.<sec>.json` fragments → `merge.ts` → `commit.ts` (`commitStaged` PRIVATE + practice) · `flip-public.ts` (ships only solved examples: `question_format='subjective' AND solution IS NOT NULL`; `--with-mcq` also flips derived-answer MCQs) · `apply-solutions.ts` (author→apply solution-only UPDATE, hash-safe, via `normalizeNewlines`; also applies `*.mcq-verify.json`) · `snap-crop.ts` + `attach-images.ts --force` (figure crop via shared `scripts/lib/figures/snapcrop.py`) · **`render_solution_diagrams.py`** (Pillow — authored SOLUTION diagrams for `diagramWouldHelp`-flagged questions; deterministic, montage-verified; **chapter-parameterized** via `SPEC_BUILDERS[chapterId]`; Pair-of-Lines specs are hardcoded, the rest are **data-driven** — authoring agents emit `data/<id>.diagram-specs*.json`. TWO renderer families: (a) **coordinate-plane** — Linear-Programming's **feasible-region mode** (Sutherland-Hodgman half-plane clip + constraint lines + corner points) and App-of-Definite-Integration's **curve mode** (`y=f(x)` exprs, conics, fill-between `shade`); (b) **physical geometry** (App-of-Derivatives — cones/boxes/ladders/windows drawn as 2-D axial cross-sections): **`segments`** (FINITE lines between two points — the coordinate-plane `lines` are infinite + viewport-clipped and useless here), `axes:false`, arc `conics` (`t0`/`t1` degrees), outlined `polys`, `rightangles`, and **`equal_aspect`** (auto-EXPANDS the viewport until px/unit matches on both axes, so circles are circular + right angles square — `px()` otherwise maps xr/yr independently; default OFF so the coordinate-plane chapters render byte-identically). Captions word-wrap. Gotcha: `polys` draw AFTER `segments`, so a closed poly overdraws a coloured highlight edge — use segments-only to highlight) + **`attach-solution-image.ts`** (ref→PNG manifest `data/<id>.solution-images.json` → sets `questions.solution_image_url`, migration 0042) · **`errata.ts`** (scans all chapters for solutions beginning `[Textbook …]` → grouped markdown **errata report for the publisher**; `--write` → `generated-papers/StateBoard_Errata.md`). **The ordered end-to-end runbook is `scripts/stateboard/README.md`** (steps 1–9), with the **answer-key cross-check against the end-of-book ANSWERS section a mandatory GATE (step 6) before `flip-public`**. See [[state-board-ingestion]] + [[textbook-chapter-ingestion]] + [[figure-snapcrop-verify]].
├── ncert/                                 NCERT textbook ingestion for **BOTH CBSE classes** — a **practice-only** corpus, same 3-bucket shape as `stateboard/` whose pure core it re-exports VERBATIM (`lib.ts` = `export * from "../stateboard/lib"`). **BOTH BOOKS COMPLETE: Class 12 all 13 Maths chapters / 1,414 q (2026-08-17); Class 11 (exam `cbse-11`) all 14 Maths chapters / 1,159 q (2026-08-18, a 4-chapter pilot then the remaining 10 in one parallel run of ~46 agents).** `dedupe-bands.ts` resolves the recurring band seam — the last worked Example before an exercise bands to the exercise it PRECEDES while being printed atop the NEXT band's first page — and REFUSES when two bands transcribed the same ref differently, since that is a finding rather than a duplicate. Two seam classes it CANNOT see (a stranded Example, a solution TAIL on the next band's page) are caught only by the brief's report-on-territory-you-do-not-own rule; `merge.ts` failing closed on a duplicate ref covers the third. **ONE pipeline, PARAMETERISED rather than forked** — `EXAM_ID` was removed as a module-level export and became a required `Chapter.examId`, so the typechecker enumerates every call site instead of a default silently scoping a Class-11 write to Class 12; forking was rejected because this repo already carries live fork-drift (`apply-errata`'s MCQ mirror hole fixed in `mh-sb-11`, still open in `stateboard/`). Class-11 chapter ids carry a `c11` prefix — Relations and Functions, Probability and Three Dimensional Geometry exist in BOTH classes and share the flat `data/` dir — and `NCERT_EXAMS` drives the cross-class aggregates (`errata.ts` sections its publisher report by exam id, since a chapter NAME does not identify a class, and prints an explicit "0 flagged items" for an empty class because a missing section reads as "not checked"). **Class-11 specifics, all measured:** its book contains **ZERO MCQs** (so `dump-mcq`/`mark-mcq-verify` have nothing to do, `mixedFormats` stays off, and `audit:keys` always reports "NOTHING SCANNED" — a false alarm, its scope being `format is null OR = 'mcq'`), its answer key `kemh1an.pdf` is **complete across all 14 chapters incl. every Miscellaneous block** (so step-6 runs on everything), and its text layer is arithmetically lossy (`√` occurs ZERO times book-wide). Extraction is **VISION-only** (scanned-with-OCR PDFs; the text layer flattens 2-D math and yields EMPTY `(A) (B) (C) (D)` for math-built options — it is good only for section→page mapping). Bands are cut by **(page, y)** off a `get_text('blocks')` probe, never by page. `config.ts` (exam id + chapter registry; each chapter carries `answerPages` into `lemh1an.pdf`/`lemh2an.pdf` — **bound it at the chapter's LAST answer, not its first**: one key spills onto a page with no heading at all, another past its own heading, and `lemh1an` p15 is SUPPLEMENTARY MATERIAL rather than answers) · `render.ts` (chapter pages; `--answers` writes to `out/_answers/<id>/`, a SIBLING of `out/<id>` which the chapter render rmSync's) · `merge.ts` (band fragments → `<id>.questions.json`; a named SCRATCH exclusion **plus a fail-CLOSED shape guard** — every row must carry a string `bucket`+`stem`, after the name filter let diagram specs through as questions) · `commit.ts` · `backfill-sections.ts` + `sections.ts` (the `/board` book-order outline) · `set-subtopic-order.ts` (`chapters` has NO `exam_id` and four of these chapter names exist under OTHER exams, so it resolves via `subjects.exam_id` and **refuses unless exactly one chapter matches**) · `dump-mcq.ts` → blind agent → `mark-mcq-verify.ts` (withholds `is_correct`; computes agreement OUTSIDE the verifier so it cannot self-report) · `dump-tosolve.ts` → authoring agents → `apply-solutions.ts` (ref→id PAIRING gate + control-char/double-escape refusal) · `dump-review.ts` → cross-check agents → **the answer-key diff is the mandatory gate** · `snap-crop.ts` + `attach-images.ts` (question figures) · `render_solution_diagrams.py` + `attach-solution-image.ts` (authored `solution_image` diagrams — ONE data-driven `build_specs(chapter)`, unlike the stateboard copy's three near-identical loaders) · `apply-errata.ts` (**must be a chapter's LAST write**; the source mirror runs INDEPENDENTLY of the DB skip so a half-applied state self-heals; count check is one-sided since brackets arrive from two sources) · `errata.ts`. Five committed agent contracts: `TRANSCRIPTION_BRIEF` · `SOLUTION_BRIEF` · `CROSSCHECK_BRIEF` · `MCQ_VERIFY_BRIEF` · `DIAGRAM_BRIEF` — **one per lane; a lane without one drifts** ([[contract-per-agent-lane]]). See [[ncert-ingestion]]. **Biology (2026-10-07, both classes):** chapters are the `BIO_11`/`BIO_12` tables + a builder in `config.ts`; contract `BIOLOGY_TRANSCRIPTION_BRIEF.md`; `set-chapter-order.ts` (order_index = book chapter number - 1, scoped exam+subject+name); `stamp-provenance.ts` stamps every row of a chapter with no `answersPdf`; `science-grounding.ts --anchors` lists what a chapter lets an author cite.
├── mh-sb-9/                               Maharashtra State Board **Class 9** (Balbharati) textbook ingestion — exam `mh-sb-9`, **practiceOnly + boardExam** (Class 9 is not a board year, so there are no PYQs *ever*, unlike `mh-ssc-10`). `lib.ts` re-exports the `stateboard/` pure core **VERBATIM** (`export * from "../stateboard/lib"`) — buckets/`buildRecords`/`assignSections` are exam-agnostic — so it is covered by `tests/stateboard-lib.test.ts` and the shipped pipeline stays untouched. Sources are **whole-book** PDFs, so every chapter sets a 0-based `pages` range in `config.ts`. **Extraction is per-subject, not per-pipeline:** Maths is VISION (unicode math flattens to `�`), the humanities books are **TEXT-FIRST** via `dump-text.ts` — which writes `out/<id>.text.md` as a **SIBLING** of `out/<id>/` because `render.ts` rmSync's that directory. `audit-grounding.ts` is the standing gate that exists because the History/PolSci book **ships no ANSWERS section at all**, so the mandatory step-6 key cross-check cannot run: it pulls every year and proper noun out of each stored answer and diffs them against the chapter's own text (TRIAGE, not proof — blind to a lowercase invention, and it says so). `attach-images.ts` carries the size-aware step-down→JPEG crop (a near-full-page colour panorama rendered 7.2 MB against the 1 MB storage cap) and accepts a pre-made `file` for a figure set that **spans a page break**. Plus `build-unit-test-geometry.ts` + `unit-test-geometry-1.ts` (a .docx unit test off the committed bank). See [[mh-sb-9-ingestion]].
├── mh-sb-11/                              Maharashtra State Board **Class 11** (Balbharati) textbook ingestion — exam `mh-sb-11`, practiceOnly + boardExam, its own `Mathematics` subject (deliberately NOT folded into `mh-hsc-12`). **COMPLETE: 18/18 chapters, 2,941 q.** `lib.ts` re-exports the `stateboard/` core verbatim. The publisher ships **pre-split per-chapter PDFs**, so `pdf` points at a whole chapter while `answersPdf` points at the whole-book volume holding the ANSWERS section (per-chapter `answerPages` index into it). **Transcription is VISION-ONLY and that is MEASURED, not assumed** — the text layer looks clean and is arithmetically lossy (`√` occurs ONCE in 21 pages of Ch.2 while the pages carry dozens of surds). `apply-solved-fixes.ts` is the piece with no sibling: it repairs an arithmetically WRONG line inside a **solved example's printed solution** — the case `apply-solutions.ts` structurally cannot reach, since that only fills rows where `solution IS NULL`, and preserving the book verbatim there would ship a model answer that teaches the error. **`scripts/mh-sb-11/README.md` is the ordered runbook** (and the source of the "cut bands at BLOCK boundaries, never page breaks" rule — a banner scan misses theory-embedded worked examples). Plus `build-unit-test.ts` + `unit-test-1.ts`. See [[mh-sb-11-ingestion]].
├── mh-ssc-10/                             Maharashtra **Class 10 (SSC) BOARD-PYQ** ingestion — exam `mh-ssc-10`, `question_kind='pyq'`, **NOT practiceOnly**: Class 10 IS a board year, so the source is actual past board question papers (scanned booklets). **Has its OWN pure core** (`lib.ts`, ~190 lines, `tests/mh-ssc-10-lib.test.ts`) rather than the stateboard one, and the reason is structural: a board PAPER spans many chapters, so each transcribed question carries its **own** chapter + subtopic — `buildPaperRecords` HARD-validates the chapter against that subject's catalog (preventing catch-all drift) while treating an off-catalog subtopic as a soft flag, because board PYQs blend topics more than a fixed exercise does. One printed paper can also carry TWO bank subjects (Social Sciences Paper I is History + Political Science), so `subject` is a PER-QUESTION field validated against its OWN subject's catalog, never the union. Flow: `render` → `dump-text` → vision/hybrid transcribe → `merge` → `commit` → `snap-crop`/`attach-images` → `flip-public`, plus `dump-answers.ts` (stored answers out for cross-checking) and `add-questions.ts` (append a few questions to an ALREADY-COMMITTED paper without disturbing it). **`build-chapter-pyq.ts` + `chapter-pyq.ts`** build a per-chapter `<chapter>_PYQ.docx` + `_PYQ_Key.docx` pair through the same `buildQuestionPaper`/`buildAnswerKey` path as a `/browse` download (56 chapters / 112 files / 1,459 q). Board QPs ship no answer key, so answers are DERIVED/AUTHORED. Runbook: `scripts/mh-ssc-10/README.md`. See [[mh-ssc-10-ingestion]].
├── mh-ssc-10-text/                        The **TEXTBOOK layer of the SAME exam** as `mh-ssc-10/` — a second pipeline writing `question_kind='practice'` into the identical exam and the identical chapters, so a chapter carries its board PYQs and its Balbharati exercises together and the `/browse` PYQ/Practice toggle separates them. `lib.ts` re-exports the `stateboard/` core verbatim (the PYQ half next door does not — see above). Born-digital text layer ⇒ **text-first** (`dump-text.ts`), not vision. Two rules were earned here: `chapterName` must match the DB row **EXACTLY** (the book prints "Historiography : Development in the West" with a space before the colon and the DB has none — the book's spelling would auto-create a duplicate chapter and split the corpus), and **subtopics are the TEXTBOOK's own section headings**, with drifted PYQ-era subtopics renamed to match so both kinds share one axis per chapter. Carries `audit-grounding.ts` (this book also ships no answers section) and **`validate-solutions.ts`**, which checks authored fragments BEFORE `apply-solutions.ts` writes them. Also holds the chapter's one-off repairs (`mask-figs.ts`, `normalise-bands.ts`, `repair-hypotenusee.ts`) and `build-unit-test-geometry.ts` + `unit-test-geometry-1.ts` (Class-10 Pythagoras). See [[mh-ssc-10-ingestion]].
├── mh-hsc-12-pyq/                         Maharashtra **HSC Class-12 BOARD PYQs** — a second pipeline into the SAME exam as `stateboard/` (`mh-hsc-12`), `question_kind='pyq'`, which is why that exam is no longer practiceOnly. **The source is an LWS-authored CHAPTERWISE COMPILATION of 15 born-digital .docx, NOT the raw papers** — that buys a clean pandoc TEXT path (no vision) and costs completeness (38-45 of a paper's 44 questions), so it is a chapterwise bank and **cannot back a `/mock` sitting**. `lib.ts` is a ~460-line pure core whose every rule exists for a **MEASURED** pandoc quirk (Phase-0 analysis; each case in `tests/mh-hsc-12-pyq-normalise.test.ts` is a real string from the source) — deliberately NOT `stateboard`'s `buildRecords`, which requires a `bucket` driving `/board`'s `section_*` book-structure axis, and a board PYQ has no place in a book's structure. Flow: `extract` (chapter → draft) → `dedupe` (reviewed cross-chapter duplicate ledger) → `assign` (reviewed subtopic assignment) → `dump` (inputs for the assignment / key-derivation / authoring passes) → `promote` (authoring output from gitignored `out/` into committed `data/`) → `merge` (reviewed keys + model answers + errata brackets) → `commit` → `attach-images` → `flip-public`. Two probes worth knowing: **`reconcile-keys.ts`** compares the TWO independent MCQ derivations and reports every disagreement **without ever picking a winner** (it also distinguishes a real conflict from stale blind input made before a stem was repaired), and **`verify-db.ts`** re-reads the committed chapter **FROM THE DATABASE** rather than trusting the commit report. `textProbes.ts` holds the corpus's text-defect probes. See [[mh-hsc-12-pyq-ingestion]].
├── ppt/                                   **TEACHING decks** (`.pptx`) — a teacher presents from these, as opposed to the question-per-slide classroom deck `/browse` downloads. Both use `src/lib/export/pptxBuilder.ts`; teaching adds `teaching` + `answer` slide kinds, and `planDeck` expands one authored `practice` entry into a question slide THEN the same question answered (a PDF cannot click-to-reveal, so the reveal is a page turn). `README.md` is the contract: the authoring line styles (`bullet`/`display`/`note`/`strong`/`warn`), the dark-on-white palette (indigo titles · red traps · green keys, all ≥7.5:1 on white; body text and every equation stay BLACK), and the five rules earned the hard way — open it in PowerPoint, then export slides to PNG and LOOK (a green suite has shipped both an unopenable file and a mis-laid-out one); a layout height estimate must be pessimistic; a figure question needs its figure or the build should FAIL; never author content through a shell heredoc. `motion-in-a-plane/` = the first set (MH State Board Std XI Physics Ch.3): `content.ts` (authored slides — 52 equations read off RENDERED pages, because that book's text layer emits U+F072 for a vector arrow and U+F024 for a hat and scrambles every displayed equation) · `build.ts` (fetches the 16 anchor PYQs LIVE by id so a later key fix flows in; `splitFigures` gives each figure its own slide; a missing figure or undownloadable anchor image is FATAL, never skipped) · `extract_figures.py` (crops the 15 numbered figures — vector art, so nothing lossless to pull — keyed on CAPTION COLOUR, column-aware, with `OVERRIDES` for the one figure the rules miss) · `figures/` (committed: the source PDF is NOT in the repo, so the build throws without them; the `_contact.png` QA montage is gitignored). Outputs go to gitignored `generated-papers/`. Six values are chapter-specific — PDF path, the chapter number in the caption regex, `COMPOSITES`, `OVERRIDES`, output filenames, the deck import; the rest generalises to Balbharati two-column typesetting (NCERT would need its caption colour RE-MEASURED). See the 2026-08-20 Decisions entry.
├── lib/needsBuild.ts                      pure build-relevance rule for the gate's CONDITIONAL `next build` (2026-08-05). `needsBuild(changedPaths)` + `buildRelevantPaths`; an **allowlist of SKIPS** (`*.md`, `tests/`, `scripts/`, `supabase/`, `generated-papers/` — verified: nothing in `src/` imports those roots) matched on the LEADING segment only, so `src/scripts/…` is still app code and any unrecognised path BUILDS. Spec: `tests/needs-build.test.ts` (27 cases). Shared by `.githooks/pre-push` + the CI `Detect build-relevant changes` step so the two gates cannot drift.
├── needs-build.ts                         CLI over the above (`npm run needs-build <base> <head>`): diffs the range, prints the verdict, exits 0 = build / 1 = skip. Fail-safe — an unresolvable base (new branch, all-zeros SHA, shallow clone, missing git) reports "build".
├── lib/needsNotesLint.ts                  pure relevance rule for the gate's CONDITIONAL `notes:lint` (2026-09-21). `needsNotesLint(changedPaths)` + `notesLintRelevantPaths` + `isNotesLintRelevant`; the **OPPOSITE polarity to needsBuild — an allowlist of RUNS** (`NOTES_LINT_ROOTS`: `src/app/notes/`, `src/lib/notes/`, the lint, `supabase/`, package.json/lock, tsconfig), because nearly every push touches `src/` so a skip-allowlist would fire on almost all of them. A run-allowlist CAN rot, so it is PINNED by `tests/notes-lint-import-roots.test.ts`, which walks the lint's real import graph. Spec: `tests/needs-notes-lint.test.ts` (26 cases)
├── lib/importGraph.ts                     pure static import-graph walker — `collectImports(entry, read)` returns every file of ours reachable through `import`/`export … from`, side-effect and dynamic imports; resolves `./`, `../` and `@/` → `src/`, tries `.ts`/`.tsx`/`index.*`; THROWS on an unresolvable relative import so a broken scan cannot pass a guard. Filesystem injected; spec on an in-memory map: `tests/import-graph.test.ts`
├── needs-notes-lint.ts                    CLI over `needsNotesLint` (`npm run needs-notes-lint <base> <head>`): exits 0 = run the lint / 1 = skip. Fail-safe — an unresolvable base runs it. Called by `.githooks/pre-push` (which picks one of the four `prepush*` scripts from this + `needs-build`) and the CI `Detect notes-lint-relevant changes` step
├── extract-taxonomy.ts                    one-shot: regenerate taxonomy.json from a reference Excel
├── seed.ts                                idempotent taxonomy seed (service-role)
├── normalize-newlines.ts                  one-shot backfill: applies `normalizeNewlines` to every question + option. Idempotent. `npx tsx scripts/normalize-newlines.ts` for dry-run; add `--apply` to write back. Not wired to an npm script — it's a one-time clean-up, not a recurring chore.
├── notes-lint.ts                          read-only integrity check for /notes editorial modules (Phase 2 — DB-tag aware). Wired to `npm run notes:lint`. Validates: (1) each note's `subtopicName` resolves under live taxonomy; (2) every `pyqExampleId` is PUBLIC AND lives in the parent module's subtopic (per [[pyqexampleid-must-match-subtopic]]); (3) every DB tag's `concept_slug` matches a known TS concept (catches orphan tags after rename); (4) each TS concept with a `pyqExampleId` has ≥1 DB-tagged question (soft warn for un-tagged bank-anchored concepts). **Foundation concepts (no `pyqExampleId`) are exempt from check 4** — they're deliberate teaching primitives, not bank-anchored, so 0 tags is expected (added 2026-05-22). **(5, soft warn, added 2026-06-02)** a concept's `authoredExample` / `selfCheckExample` should be a DIFFERENT problem from its featured PYQ — fetches the PYQ text and flags when the example re-uses ≥80% of its distinctive numbers (≥5 shared OR ≥2 distinctive: |n|≥5, multi-digit, decimal). Subscripts are stripped first (`I_2`, `C_{ij}` are indices, not magnitudes); superscript powers kept. Number-based, so strong on computational dups, weak on word/variable-only ones. Heuristic is the pure `reusesPyqNumbers` in `src/lib/notes/pyqDuplication.ts` (unit-tested, `tests/notes-pyq-duplication.test.ts`) — see [[notes-concept-content-alignment]]. **Static pre-checks (no-DB):** GLOBAL subtopic-slug uniqueness (0); and every `tier:"paid"` chapter's `[subtopicSlug]/page.tsx` MUST `export const dynamic = "force-dynamic"` and NOT export `revalidate` (0b, added 2026-06-01 — the preview-gate reads cookies, so a statically-cached anon render would leak the preview). Exits non-zero on error (WARNs don't block) so it can gate CI later.
├── notes-coverage.ts                    triage probe (`npm run notes:coverage [subjectRoute] [chapterSlug]`, default nda-maths statistics): per-subtopic diff of math in PUBLIC question SOLUTIONS vs notes bodies — flags macros + operand-abstracted structural fragments (`(20^2-1)/12` ≡ `(n^2-1)/12`) appearing in ≥2 solutions but 0 notes (formula-gap candidates). Notes-vocab spans `\(...\)` zones AND raw `formula.latex`/reference-cells (outside delimiters — missing them false-flags, e.g. `\prod`/GM). Macro channel = high-signal; fragments = noisy generic-arithmetic. Human-review list, NOT a gate; finds ABSENCE not under-emphasis. Read-only.
├── sync-subtopic-order.ts               populate `subtopics.order_index` (migration 0029) from each /notes chapter's `subtopicOrder` — the teaching order /browse sorts subtopics + chapter-scoped questions by. Wired to `npm run notes:order`. Idempotent; `--dry` reports without writing. LOUD on the name-join caveat: reports any notes `subtopicName` with no live DB subtopic AND any DB subtopic in a noted chapter left unordered (would sort ahead of its ordered siblings). Run as a post-ship step when a notes chapter lands or a noted chapter's subtopics are re-carved. Only the ~11 noted chapters get an order; all others keep `order_index` NULL → historical count-desc.
├── notes-latex-audit.ts                  read-only LaTeX audit of /notes editorial content. Wired to `npm run notes:latex`. Walks every field, classifies plain-text vs KaTeX-rendered, and reports: (1) LaTeX **or Markdown (`**bold**` / `- ` bullets)** leaking into the 7 plain-text fields — the 5 SubtopicNote/ConceptUnit fields (title/oneLineDefinition/whyItMatters/concept.name/formula.label) PLUS the 2 ChapterNote fields (chapter.title/chapter.intro, added 2026-05-30 after `**bold**` leaked into a chapter landing's GuideHero), (2) unbalanced `\(...\)` / `\[...\]` delimiters (strips `\\` line-breaks first so `\\[4pt]` row-spacing isn't a false positive), (3) a non-ASCII inventory (informational — prose unicode is fine). Exits non-zero on (1) or (2). Added 2026-05-29; Markdown + ChapterNote-field checks added 2026-05-30.
├── audit-voice.ts                        em-dash (AI-voice) report, `npm run audit:voice`: rate per site area + worst files, `-- <path>` lists every hit, `-- --update` rewrites the ratchet baseline `scripts/voice/dash-baseline.json`, `-- --solutions` reports PUBLIC solutions per exam (read-only). Pure core `scripts/lib/voiceProbe.ts` (TypeScript parser, so comments never count); spec `tests/voice-probe.test.ts`; gate `tests/voice-ratchet.test.ts` (no file may gain a dash). Added 2026-10-05.
└── audit-underline-patterns.ts            read-only audit of `\underline{...}` payloads across every PUBLIC question's text/context/solution + every option. Wired to `npm run audit:underlines`. Classifies each hit as **covered** (matches `UNDERLINE_BYPASS_RE` in `src/lib/export/ommlBuilder.ts` → renders as native Word `<w:u>`) or **fall-through** (still routes through OMML borderBox, which Word doesn't reliably render). Run after each new English/Biology batch — silent fall-through is the failure mode. Live state 2026-05-26: 639/639 payloads covered.

tests/                                     ~125 .test.ts files, ~1160 tests (papers: `papers-template`/`papers-sections` pure helpers (41 — incl. positionForMove reorder + planBulkAdd cart-commit) + `papers-rls` RLS/data integration (10) — migration 0039)
├── fixtures/{upload,tinyImage}.ts         in-memory .xlsx fixture builder; 67-byte 1x1 PNG buffer
├── global-teardown.ts                     vitest globalSetup: returns a teardown that sweeps test-prefixed taxonomy (PYQB_/QD_/UD_/EditTest…) + non-canonical auto-creates ("Chemical Thermodynamics" under Chemistry, "Chain Rule" under Differentiation) once after the whole run finishes. Safety net for crashed afterAll hooks; complements per-suite cleanup of run-id-scoped subjects.
├── *.test.ts                              pure unit + DB integration (DB tests skip if env missing)
└── setup.ts                               loads .env.local for tests
```

> **`browse-query.test.ts` is now exam-scoped.** The legacy `expect(totalCount).toBe(150)` assumed LWS Pune held exactly the seeded 150 MHT-CET questions; once admins started uploading additional batches (NDA, more MHT-CET) the count grew. Tests now filter by `examId = MHT-CET` and assert `>= 150` for the seeded baseline rather than an exact number.
